"""AI client that survives free-tier problems.

Idea: never depend on ONE model. Several providers (all speak the same OpenAI-style API) are tried
in order. If a model was retired (404), rate limited (429), has a bad key (401) or times out,
it is skipped for a while and the next one is tried. If everything fails, AIUnavailable is raised and
the app still returns the rule-engine results, marked ai_status="degraded".

Add or change providers/models with environment variables only, no code edits:
  GROQ_MODELS=openai/gpt-oss-120b,qwen/qwen3.6-27b      (comma list, tried in order)
  GEMINI_MODELS=gemini-3.5-flash,gemini-3.1-flash-lite
  OPENROUTER_MODELS=openrouter/free
  CUSTOM_BASE_URL / CUSTOM_API_KEY / CUSTOM_MODELS       (any other OpenAI-compatible service)
  AI_PROVIDER_ORDER=groq,gemini,openrouter,custom
"""
import base64
import json
import os
import re
import threading
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

import httpx

PROVIDERS = [
    {"name": "groq", "base_url": "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1", "key_env": "GROQ_API_KEY",
     "models_env": "GROQ_MODELS", "default_models": "openai/gpt-oss-120b,qwen/qwen3.6-27b,openai/gpt-oss-20b",
     "vision_env": None, "default_vision": ""},
    {"name": "gemini", "base_url": "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/openai", "key_env": "GEMINI_API_KEY",
     "models_env": "GEMINI_MODELS", "default_models": "gemini-3.5-flash,gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
     "vision_env": "GEMINI_VISION_MODELS", "default_vision": "gemini-3.5-flash"},
    {"name": "openrouter", "base_url": "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1", "key_env": "OPENROUTER_API_KEY",
     "models_env": "OPENROUTER_MODELS", "default_models": "openrouter/free",
     "vision_env": "OPENROUTER_VISION_MODELS", "default_vision": "openrouter/free"},
    {"name": "custom", "base_url": "", "base_url_env": "CUSTOM_BASE_URL", "key_env": "CUSTOM_API_KEY",
     "models_env": "CUSTOM_MODELS", "default_models": "",
     "vision_env": "CUSTOM_VISION_MODELS", "default_vision": ""},
]

PER_CALL_TIMEOUT = float(os.getenv("AI_CALL_TIMEOUT", "12.0"))
TOTAL_BUDGET = float(os.getenv("AI_TOTAL_TIMEOUT", "30"))
AI_MODE = os.getenv("AI_MODE", "auto").strip().lower()
AI_DAILY_LIMIT = int(os.getenv("AI_DAILY_LIMIT", "1500"))
_daily_calls = 0
_daily_reset_day = int(time.time() // 86400)
_daily_lock = threading.Lock()
NUDGE = "\n\nIMPORTANT: your previous reply was not valid JSON. Reply with ONE valid JSON object only. No markdown, no commentary."

# model names that are never chat models (used only by auto-discovery)
_GOOD = re.compile(r"(gpt-oss|llama|qwen|gemini|mistral|deepseek|gemma)", re.I)
_BAD = re.compile(r"(whisper|tts|guard|embed|image|audio|live|moderation|safeguard|orpheus|robotics|imagen|veo|omni)", re.I)

_cooldown: Dict[Tuple[str, str], float] = {}
_successful_model_by_provider: Dict[str, str] = {}
_successful_provider: Optional[str] = None


class AIUnavailable(Exception):
    """Every configured provider/model failed."""


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- tiny HTTP wrappers (tests replace these)
_http_client = httpx.Client(
    timeout=PER_CALL_TIMEOUT,
    limits=httpx.Limits(max_keepalive_connections=10, max_connections=20, keepalive_expiry=60.0),
)


def _post(url: str, headers: dict, payload: dict, timeout: float) -> Tuple[int, str]:
    r = _http_client.post(url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=timeout)
    return r.status_code, r.text


def _get(url: str, headers: dict, timeout: float) -> Tuple[int, str]:
    r = _http_client.get(url, headers=headers, timeout=timeout)
    return r.status_code, r.text


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- configuration
def _clean(value: Optional[str]) -> str:
    return (value or "").strip().strip('"').strip("'")


def _configured(p: dict, vision: bool = False) -> Optional[dict]:
    key = _clean(os.getenv(p["key_env"]))
    if not key or "your_" in key.lower():
        return None
    base = _clean(os.getenv(p["base_url_env"])) if p.get("base_url_env") else p["base_url"]
    if not base:
        return None
    if vision:
        if not p.get("vision_env"):
            return None
        raw = os.getenv(p["vision_env"], p["default_vision"])
    else:
        raw = os.getenv(p["models_env"], p["default_models"])
    models = [m.strip() for m in raw.split(",") if m.strip()]
    if not models:
        return None
    return {**p, "key": key, "base_url": base.rstrip("/"), "models": models}


def _ordered_providers(vision: bool = False) -> List[dict]:
    order = [x.strip() for x in os.getenv("AI_PROVIDER_ORDER", "groq,gemini,openrouter,custom").split(",") if x.strip()]
    by_name = {p["name"]: p for p in PROVIDERS}
    result = []
    for name in order:
        if name in by_name:
            cfg = _configured(by_name[name], vision)
            if cfg:
                successful_model = _successful_model_by_provider.get(name) if not vision else None
                if successful_model and successful_model in cfg["models"]:
                    cfg["models"] = [successful_model] + [m for m in cfg["models"] if m != successful_model]
                result.append(cfg)
    if not vision and _successful_provider:
        result.sort(key=lambda provider: provider["name"] != _successful_provider)
    return result


def has_any_provider() -> bool:
    return bool(_ordered_providers())


def _on_cooldown(provider: str, model: str) -> bool:
    now = time.monotonic()
    return _cooldown.get((provider, model), 0) > now or _cooldown.get((provider, "*"), 0) > now


def _set_cooldown(provider: str, model: str, seconds: float) -> None:
    _cooldown[(provider, model)] = time.monotonic() + seconds


def reset_cooldowns() -> None:
    _cooldown.clear()


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- one HTTP call
def _extract_json(text: str) -> Any:
    text = re.sub(r"<think>.*?</think>", "", text or "", flags=re.S)
    text = re.sub(r"```(?:json)?", "", text).strip()
    try:
        return json.loads(text)
    except Exception:
        start, end = text.find("{"), text.rfind("}")
        if start != -1 and end > start:
            return json.loads(text[start:end + 1])
        raise


def _call_model(p: dict, model: str, system: str, user: str, image: Optional[Tuple[bytes, str]],
                max_tokens: int, timeout: float, json_mode: bool = True, temperature: float = 0.0):
    """Returns (content_text, None) on success or (None, (kind, message)) on failure.
    kind: 'auth' | 'missing' | 'rate' | 'error'"""
    if image:
        b64 = base64.b64encode(image[0]).decode()
        user_content: Any = [
            {"type": "text", "text": user},
            {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": f"data:{image[1]};base64,{b64}"}},
        ]
    else:
        user_content = user
    payload = {
        "model": model.replace("models/", ""),
        "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": system}, {"role": "user", "content": user_content}],
        "temperature": temperature,
        "max_tokens": max_tokens,
    }
    if json_mode:
        payload["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {p['key']}", "Content-Type": "application/json"}
    try:
        status, body = _post(p["base_url"] + "/chat/completions", headers, payload, timeout)
    except Exception as e:  # timeouts, DNS, connection resets
        return None, ("error", f"{type(e).__name__}")
    if status == 200:
        try:
            content = json.loads(body)["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
            if isinstance(content, list):
                content = "".join(part.get("text", "") for part in content if isinstance(part, dict))
            if not content or not str(content).strip():
                return None, ("error", "empty reply")
            return str(content), None
        except Exception:
            return None, ("error", "unreadable response")
    if status == 400 and json_mode:  # this model may not support JSON mode -> retry without it
        return _call_model(p, model, system, user, image, max_tokens, timeout, json_mode=False, temperature=temperature)
    snippet = body[:160].replace("\n", " ")
    if status in (401, 403):
        return None, ("auth", f"HTTP {status} (check the API key)")
    if status == 404:
        return None, ("missing", f"HTTP 404 (model retired or renamed)")
    if status == 429:
        return None, ("rate", "HTTP 429 (rate limited)")
    return None, ("error", f"HTTP {status} {snippet}")


def _apply_cooldown(p: dict, model: str, kind: str) -> None:
    if kind == "auth":
        _set_cooldown(p["name"], "*", 600)
    elif kind == "missing":
        _set_cooldown(p["name"], model, 3600)
    elif kind == "rate":
        _set_cooldown(p["name"], model, 60)
    else:
        _set_cooldown(p["name"], model, 30)


def _attempt(p: dict, model: str, system: str, user: str, image, max_tokens: int, deadline: float, errors: List[str]):
    """One model, with one JSON-repair retry. Returns (parsed_json | None, kind)."""
    for attempt in range(2):
        call_limit = max(PER_CALL_TIMEOUT, 12.0) if image is not None else PER_CALL_TIMEOUT
        timeout = min(call_limit, deadline - time.monotonic())
        if timeout < 1:
            errors.append("time budget used up")
            return None, "timeout"
        content, fail = _call_model(p, model, system if attempt == 0 else system + NUDGE, user, image,
                                    max_tokens, timeout, temperature=0.0 if attempt == 0 else 0.3)
        if fail:
            kind, msg = fail
            errors.append(f"{p['name']}/{model}: {msg}")
            _apply_cooldown(p, model, kind)
            return None, kind
        try:
            data = _extract_json(content)
            if isinstance(data, (dict, list)):
                return data, "ok"
        except Exception:
            pass
        errors.append(f"{p['name']}/{model}: reply was not valid JSON")
    _set_cooldown(p["name"], model, 30)
    return None, "parse"


def _discover_models(p: dict, vision: bool) -> List[str]:
    """Last resort when every configured model is gone: ask the provider what it offers now."""
    try:
        status, body = _get(p["base_url"] + "/models", {"Authorization": f"Bearer {p['key']}"}, 10)
        if status != 200:
            return []
        items = json.loads(body).get("data", [])
        ids = [str(i.get("id", "")).replace("models/", "") for i in items]
        picks = [i for i in ids if i and _GOOD.search(i) and not _BAD.search(i)]
        if vision:
            picks = [i for i in picks if re.search(r"(gemini|vision|vl)", i, re.I)]
        return picks[:2]
    except Exception:
        return []


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- public API
def complete_json(system: str, user: str, image: Optional[Tuple[bytes, str]] = None,
                  max_tokens: int = 3000) -> Tuple[Any, str]:
    """Returns (parsed_json, 'provider/model'). Raises AIUnavailable if nothing works."""
    global _daily_calls, _daily_reset_day
    if AI_MODE == "rules_only":
        raise AIUnavailable("AI_MODE is set to rules_only (deterministic mode)")
    today = int(time.time() // 86400)
    with _daily_lock:
        if today != _daily_reset_day:
            _daily_calls = 0
            _daily_reset_day = today
        if _daily_calls >= AI_DAILY_LIMIT:
            raise AIUnavailable("Daily AI call budget reached; running in deterministic mode")
        _daily_calls += 1
    deadline = time.monotonic() + TOTAL_BUDGET
    providers = _ordered_providers(vision=image is not None)
    if not providers:
        raise AIUnavailable("no AI API key configured")
    errors: List[str] = []
    global _successful_provider
    for p in providers:
        if _on_cooldown(p["name"], "*"):
            errors.append(f"{p['name']}: skipped (cooling down)")
            continue
        candidates = [m for m in p["models"] if not _on_cooldown(p["name"], m)]
        missing = 0
        for model in candidates:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                raise AIUnavailable("; ".join(errors[-6:]) or "time budget used up")
            data, kind = _attempt(p, model, system, user, image, max_tokens, deadline, errors)
            if kind == "ok":
                _successful_model_by_provider[p["name"]] = model
                if image is None:
                    _successful_provider = p["name"]
                return data, f"{p['name']}/{model}"
            if kind == "auth":
                break  # bad key: no point trying this provider's other models
            if kind == "missing":
                missing += 1
        if candidates and missing == len(candidates) and not _on_cooldown(p["name"], "*"):
            for model in _discover_models(p, vision=image is not None):
                data, kind = _attempt(p, model, system, user, image, max_tokens, deadline, errors)
                if kind == "ok":
                    return data, f"{p['name']}/{model} (auto-discovered)"
    raise AIUnavailable("; ".join(errors[-6:]) or "all providers failed")


def budget_info() -> dict:
    global _daily_calls, _daily_reset_day
    today = int(time.time() // 86400)
    with _daily_lock:
        if today != _daily_reset_day:
            _daily_calls = 0
            _daily_reset_day = today
        return {"ai_mode": AI_MODE, "daily_calls_used": _daily_calls, "daily_limit": AI_DAILY_LIMIT}


def status() -> List[dict]:
    """Live diagnostic for /api/ai-status: tests each provider with a tiny request."""
    report = []
    for p in PROVIDERS:
        cfg = _configured(p)
        if not cfg:
            report.append({"provider": p["name"], "configured": False})
            continue
        entry = {"provider": p["name"], "configured": True, "working_model": None, "failures": []}
        for model in cfg["models"]:
            content, fail = _call_model(cfg, model, "Reply with JSON only.", 'Return exactly: {"ok": true}', None, 200, 15)
            if not fail:
                entry["working_model"] = model
                break
            entry["failures"].append({"model": model, "problem": fail[1]})
        report.append(entry)
    return report

"""Local TestClient latency benchmark for ClaimLoom Round 3 endpoints."""
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import os
import statistics
import time

try:
    import pymupdf as fitz
except ImportError:
    import fitz
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

import ai_client
import main

client = TestClient(main.app)
ITERATIONS = 10


def make_pdf() -> bytes:
    doc = fitz.open()
    for page_no in range(2):
        page = doc.new_page()
        page.insert_text((50, 70), f"ClaimLoom brochure page {page_no + 1}: verified battery range is 110 km.")
    result = doc.tobytes()
    doc.close()
    return result


def measure(name, action):
    timings = []
    for iteration in range(ITERATIONS):
        started = time.perf_counter()
        action(iteration)
        timings.append((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000)
    ordered = sorted(timings)
    p95 = ordered[max(0, min(len(ordered) - 1, int(0.95 * len(ordered) + 0.999) - 1))]
    return {"name": name, "p50": statistics.median(ordered), "p95": p95, "min": ordered[0], "max": ordered[-1]}


def main_benchmark():
    os.environ["AI_MODE"] = "rules_only"
    ai_client.AI_MODE = "rules_only"
    tokens = {}
    for role in ("Creator", "Reviewer"):
        response = client.post("/api/session", json={"name": f"Benchmark {role}", "role": role, "workspace_id": "default"})
        response.raise_for_status()
        tokens[role] = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {response.json()['token']}"}

    metrics = []

    def uncached_text(i):
        response = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": f"Deterministic benchmark run {i}: ClaimLoom supports compliant enterprise teams."}, headers=tokens["Creator"])
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json().get("cached") is False

    metrics.append(measure("Uncached deterministic text scan", uncached_text))

    # Exercise the normal successful-AI cache path without making network calls.
    previous_mode, previous_complete = ai_client.AI_MODE, ai_client.complete_json
    ai_client.AI_MODE = "auto"
    ai_client.complete_json = lambda *args, **kwargs: ({"candidates": []}, "benchmark/mock")
    try:
        def cached_text(i):
            text = f"Cache benchmark run {i}: ClaimLoom supports compliant enterprise teams."
            first = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": text}, headers=tokens["Creator"])
            assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
            second = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": text}, headers=tokens["Creator"])
            assert second.status_code == 200 and second.json().get("cached") is True, second.text
        metrics.append(measure("Cached text scan", cached_text))
    finally:
        ai_client.complete_json = previous_complete
        ai_client.AI_MODE = previous_mode

    pdf_bytes = make_pdf()
    pdf_ids = []

    def pdf_scan(i):
        response = client.post("/api/scan-pdf", files={"file": (f"bench-{i}.pdf", pdf_bytes, "application/pdf")}, data={"workspace_id": "default"}, headers=tokens["Creator"])
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        data = response.json()
        assert data["page_count"] == 2
        assert all(isinstance(issue.get("bbox"), list) and len(issue["bbox"]) == 4 for issue in data["issues"])
        pdf_ids.append(data["scan_id"])

    metrics.append(measure("2-page PDF scan + bbox extraction", pdf_scan))

    signed_ids = []

    def redline_export(i):
        response = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": f"Cryptographic certificate benchmark {i}: ClaimLoom helps enterprise teams."}, headers=tokens["Creator"])
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        scan_id = response.json()["scan_id"]
        approval = client.post(f"/api/scans/{scan_id}/approve", json={}, headers=tokens["Reviewer"])
        assert approval.status_code == 200, approval.text
        redline = client.get(f"/api/scans/{scan_id}/redline-pdf")
        assert redline.status_code == 200 and redline.content.startswith(b"%PDF"), redline.text[:200]
        red_doc = fitz.open(stream=redline.content, filetype="pdf")
        try:
            assert "CRYPTOGRAPHIC COMPLIANCE & AUDIT SEAL" in red_doc[-1].get_text()
        finally:
            red_doc.close()
        signed_ids.append(scan_id)

    metrics.append(measure("Redlined PDF export + certificate", redline_export))

    verify_index = 0

    def verify_signature(_i):
        nonlocal verify_index
        scan_id = signed_ids[verify_index]
        verify_index += 1
        response = client.get(f"/api/verify/{scan_id}")
        assert response.status_code == 200 and response.json().get("valid") is True, response.text

    metrics.append(measure("Ed25519 approval signature verification", verify_signature))

    print("| Operation | p50 (ms) | p95 (ms) | Min/Max (ms) |")
    print("|---|---:|---:|---:|")
    for row in metrics:
        print(f"| {row['name']} | {row['p50']:.2f} | {row['p95']:.2f} | {row['min']:.2f} / {row['max']:.2f} |")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main_benchmark()


========================================================================================
FILE: cache.py
========================================================================================
"""SHA-256 Deterministic + Semantic Scan Cache.
Computes SHA-256 over (text + channel + workspace_id + rules_version + kb_version).
Backed by Neon's `scan_cache(cache_key PK, response_json JSONB, created_at)` table
with a bounded in-memory LRU fallback.
CRITICAL RULE: Never caches or serves responses where ai_status != 'ok'."""
import copy
import hashlib
import json
import threading
from collections import OrderedDict
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

import db
from schemas import normalize_channel

MAX_MEMORY_CACHE_ITEMS = 200  # Bounded for Render 512 MB RAM
_mem_lock = threading.Lock()
_mem_cache: "OrderedDict[str, Dict[str, Any]]" = OrderedDict()
_known_misses: set[str] = set()
_cache_warm = False


def make_cache_key(
    text: str,
    channel: str = "general",
    workspace_id: str = "default",
    rules_version: int = 1,
    kb_version: int = 1,
) -> str:
    """Builds a deterministic SHA-256 hex digest over normalized scan inputs."""
    norm_text = (text or "").strip()
    norm_ch = normalize_channel(channel)
    norm_ws = (workspace_id or "default").strip() or "default"
    raw = f"v3|{norm_ws}|{norm_ch}|r:{int(rules_version)}|kb:{int(kb_version)}|{norm_text}"
    return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _mem_get(cache_key: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    with _mem_lock:
        item = _mem_cache.get(cache_key)
        if item is not None:
            _mem_cache.move_to_end(cache_key)
            return copy.deepcopy(item)
        return None


def _mem_set(cache_key: str, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    with _mem_lock:
        _mem_cache[cache_key] = copy.deepcopy(payload)
        _mem_cache.move_to_end(cache_key)
        while len(_mem_cache) > MAX_MEMORY_CACHE_ITEMS:
            _mem_cache.popitem(last=False)


def get_cached_scan(cache_key: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Looks up a cached ScanResponse dict by cache_key.
    Returns a copy with `cached=True`, or None on miss / degraded status."""
    # 1. Fast path: check in-memory LRU first (saves Neon compute & latency)
    mem_hit = _mem_get(cache_key)
    if mem_hit and mem_hit.get("ai_status") == "ok":
        mem_hit["cached"] = True
        return mem_hit

    global _cache_warm
    with _mem_lock:
        if cache_key in _known_misses:
            return None

    # 2. Check Neon `scan_cache` table
    try:
        conn = db._connect()
        if not conn:
            with _mem_lock:
                _known_misses.add(cache_key)
                _cache_warm = True
            return None
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "SELECT response_json FROM public.scan_cache WHERE cache_key = %s;",
                    (cache_key,),
                )
                row = cur.fetchone()
        finally:
            conn.close()

        if not row or not row[0]:
            with _mem_lock:
                _known_misses.add(cache_key)
                _cache_warm = True
            return None

        data = row[0]
        if isinstance(data, str):
            data = json.loads(data)
        if not isinstance(data, dict) or data.get("ai_status") != "ok":
            return None

        data["cached"] = True
        _mem_set(cache_key, data)
        with _mem_lock:
            _cache_warm = True
        return copy.deepcopy(data)
    except Exception as e:
        with _mem_lock:
            _known_misses.add(cache_key)
            _cache_warm = True
        print(f"[cache] get_cached_scan fallback (table may not exist yet): {e}")
        return None


def set_cached_scan(cache_key: str, response_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """Stores a ScanResponse dict in memory and Neon `scan_cache`.
    Skips caching if `ai_status != 'ok'`."""
    if not isinstance(response_data, dict):
        return False
    if response_data.get("ai_status") != "ok":
        return False

    to_store = copy.deepcopy(response_data)
    to_store["cached"] = True
    _mem_set(cache_key, to_store)
    with _mem_lock:
        try:
            _known_misses.discard(cache_key)
        except AttributeError:
            _known_misses.pop(cache_key, None)

    def _persist_cache_entry() -> None:
        try:
            conn = db._connect()
            if not conn:
                return
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO public.scan_cache (cache_key, response_json)
                       VALUES (%s, %s::jsonb)
                       ON CONFLICT (cache_key) DO UPDATE SET response_json = EXCLUDED.response_json,
                       created_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;""",
                    (cache_key, json.dumps(to_store)),
                )
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[cache] set_cached_scan Neon write skipped: {exc}")

    threading.Thread(target=_persist_cache_entry, daemon=True, name="scan-cache-persist").start()
    return True


def invalidate_cache() -> None:
    """Clears in-memory and Neon scan_cache when brand rules or KB facts are modified."""
    global _cache_warm
    with _mem_lock:
        _mem_cache.clear()
        _known_misses.clear()
        _cache_warm = False

    def _delete_neon_cache() -> None:
        try:
            conn = db._connect()
            if not conn:
                return
            try:
                with conn.cursor() as cur:
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM public.scan_cache;")
                conn.commit()
            finally:
                conn.close()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[cache] invalidate_cache Neon delete skipped: {e}")

    threading.Thread(target=_delete_neon_cache, daemon=True, name="scan-cache-invalidate").start()


========================================================================================
FILE: collab_router.py
========================================================================================
"""Review comments, approvals, and public verification endpoints."""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import hashlib
import json
from collections import Counter
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

import db
import signing
from schemas import Issue, score_issues
from session import SessionUser, get_current_session, require_role

router = APIRouter()

# Useful for local development and when Neon is unavailable; production writes go to Neon.
_comments: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
_approvals: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
_approvals_loaded = False
_memory_versions: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}


def _sync_mem_scan(scan_id: str, data: dict) -> None:
    """Keep the shared scan lookup caches aligned with scan mutations."""
    try:
        import main
        main._mem_scans[scan_id] = data
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        import pdf_engine
        pdf_engine._mem_scan_json[scan_id] = data
    except Exception:
        pass


def _safe_connect():
    try:
        return db._connect()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"[collab_router] database connection fallback: {exc}")
        return None


class CommentRequest(BaseModel):
    comment_text: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=4000)


class ApprovalRequest(BaseModel):
    version_no: Optional[int] = Field(default=None, ge=1)


class VersionRequest(BaseModel):
    text_snapshot: str = Field(..., max_length=20000)
    score: Optional[int] = Field(default=None, ge=0, le=100)


def _scan_data(scan_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        import pdf_engine
        scan = pdf_engine._load_scan_json(scan_id)
        if scan:
            return scan
    except Exception:
        pass
    conn = _safe_connect()
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                try:
                    cur.execute("SELECT result_json, redacted_text, reaudit_needed, reaudit_reason FROM public.scan_documents WHERE scan_id = %s", (scan_id,))
                    has_reaudit_reason = True
                except Exception:
                    # Keep compatibility with databases that have not yet had
                    # the additive reaudit_reason migration applied.
                    conn.rollback()
                    cur.execute("SELECT result_json, redacted_text, reaudit_needed FROM public.scan_documents WHERE scan_id = %s", (scan_id,))
                    has_reaudit_reason = False
                row = cur.fetchone()
                if row:
                    result = row[0] if isinstance(row[0], dict) else json.loads(row[0])
                    result["redacted_text"] = row[1]
                    if row[2]:
                        note = row[3] if has_reaudit_reason else None
                        result["reaudit"] = {"needed": True, "note": note or "A cited source changed; this scan needs a new audit."}
                        result["status"] = "needs_reaudit"
                    return result
                cur.execute("SELECT payload FROM public.scans WHERE scan_id = %s", (scan_id,))
                row = cur.fetchone()
                if row and row[0]:
                    return row[0] if isinstance(row[0], dict) else json.loads(row[0])
        except Exception:
            pass
        finally:
            conn.close()
    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Scan {scan_id} not found.")


def _iso(value: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        # PostgreSQL TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE values come back naive. Our
        # approval timestamps are created in UTC, so interpret those as UTC
        # instead of letting the host's local timezone change the signed value.
        if value.tzinfo is None:
            value = value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    return str(value)


def _check_scan_workspace(scan: Dict[str, Any], session: SessionUser) -> None:
    scan_workspace = str(scan.get("workspace_id") or "default")
    if scan_workspace != session.workspace_id and session.role != "Admin":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Scan not found.")


def _latest_version(scan_id: str, scan: Dict[str, Any], requested: Optional[int]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    versions = _memory_versions.get(scan_id)
    if versions is not None:
        candidates = [v for v in versions if requested is None or int(v["version_no"]) == requested]
        if candidates:
            return dict(candidates[-1])
        if requested not in (None, 1):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Scan version {requested} not found.")
        if requested == 1 and not versions:
            return {"version_no": 1, "text_snapshot": scan.get("redacted_text") or "", "score": int(scan.get("readiness_score") or 0)}
        if requested is None and not versions:
            return {"version_no": 1, "text_snapshot": scan.get("redacted_text") or "", "score": int(scan.get("readiness_score") or 0)}
        # Memory is authoritative once initialized; don't fetch the same rows repeatedly.
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Scan version {requested} not found.")

    conn = _safe_connect()
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT version_no, text_snapshot, score, actor_name, created_at FROM public.scan_versions WHERE scan_id = %s ORDER BY version_no ASC", (scan_id,))
                rows = cur.fetchall()
                if rows:
                    versions = [{"version_no": int(r[0]), "text_snapshot": r[1] or "", "score": int(r[2] or 0), "actor_name": r[3], "created_at": _iso(r[4])} for r in rows]
                    _memory_versions[scan_id] = versions
                    candidates = [v for v in versions if requested is None or v["version_no"] == requested]
                    if candidates:
                        return dict(candidates[-1])
        except Exception:
            pass
        finally:
            conn.close()
    versions = _memory_versions.get(scan_id, [])
    if versions:
        candidates = [v for v in versions if requested is None or v["version_no"] == requested]
        if candidates:
            return dict(candidates[-1])
    if requested not in (None, 1):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Scan version {requested} not found.")
    return {
        "version_no": 1,
        "text_snapshot": scan.get("redacted_text") or "",
        "score": int(scan.get("readiness_score") or 0),
    }


@router.post("/api/scans/{scan_id}/versions")
def create_scan_version(
    scan_id: str,
    req: VersionRequest,
    session: SessionUser = Depends(get_current_session),
):
    """Store an applied-fix snapshot for the reviewer's before/after diff."""
    scan = _scan_data(scan_id)
    _check_scan_workspace(scan, session)
    current = _latest_version(scan_id, scan, None)
    next_version = current["version_no"] + 1
    normalized_text = " ".join(req.text_snapshot.casefold().split())
    active_issue_models = []
    for raw_issue in scan.get("issues") or []:
        try:
            issue = Issue.model_validate(raw_issue)
            needle = " ".join(issue.flagged_text.casefold().split())
            if needle and needle in normalized_text:
                active_issue_models.append(issue)
        except Exception:
            continue
    calculated_score, calculated_pillars, calculated_status = score_issues(
        active_issue_models, ai_ok=scan.get("ai_status", "ok") == "ok"
    )
    updated = {
        **scan,
        "redacted_text": req.text_snapshot,
        "readiness_score": calculated_score,
        "pillar_scores": calculated_pillars.model_dump(),
        "status": calculated_status,
        "issues": [issue.model_dump() for issue in active_issue_models],
    }
    version_record = {
        "version_no": next_version,
        "text_snapshot": req.text_snapshot,
        "score": calculated_score,
        "actor_name": session.name,
        "created_at": signing.utc_timestamp(),
    }
    _memory_versions.setdefault(scan_id, []).append(version_record)
    _sync_mem_scan(scan_id, updated)

    def _persist_version() -> None:
        conn = _safe_connect()
        if not conn:
            return
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO public.scan_versions (scan_id, version_no, text_snapshot, score, actor_name) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)",
                    (scan_id, next_version, req.text_snapshot, calculated_score, session.name),
                )
                cur.execute(
                    "UPDATE public.scan_documents SET redacted_text = %s, result_json = %s::jsonb WHERE scan_id = %s",
                    (req.text_snapshot, json.dumps(updated), scan_id),
                )
                cur.execute(
                    "UPDATE public.scans SET readiness_score = %s, payload = %s::jsonb WHERE scan_id = %s",
                    (calculated_score, json.dumps(updated), scan_id),
                )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            conn.rollback()
            print(f"[collab_router] scan version persistence skipped: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()

    import threading
    threading.Thread(target=_persist_version, daemon=True, name="scan-version-persist").start()
    return {"scan_id": scan_id, "version_no": next_version, "text_snapshot": req.text_snapshot, "score": calculated_score, "actor_name": session.name}


def _approval_rows(scan_id: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    global _approvals_loaded
    if not _approvals_loaded:
        conn = _safe_connect()
    else:
        conn = None
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                query = "SELECT approval_id, scan_id, version_no, text_sha256, score, reviewer, role, prev_hash, entry_hash, signature, created_at FROM public.approvals"
                params: tuple = ()
                if scan_id is not None:
                    query += " WHERE scan_id = %s"
                    params = (scan_id,)
                query += " ORDER BY created_at ASC, approval_id ASC"
                cur.execute(query, params)
                rows = cur.fetchall()
                rows_out = [
                    {"approval_id": r[0], "scan_id": r[1], "version_no": int(r[2]), "text_sha256": r[3], "score": int(r[4]),
                     "reviewer": r[5], "role": r[6], "prev_hash": r[7], "entry_hash": r[8], "signature": r[9], "created_at": _iso(r[10])}
                    for r in rows
                ]
                _approvals[:] = rows_out
                _approvals_loaded = True
        except Exception:
            pass
        finally:
            conn.close()
    if not _approvals_loaded:
        _approvals_loaded = True
    rows = list(_approvals)
    return [a for a in rows if scan_id is None or a["scan_id"] == scan_id]


@router.post("/api/scans/{scan_id}/comments")
def add_comment(scan_id: str, req: CommentRequest, session: SessionUser = Depends(get_current_session)):
    _check_scan_workspace(_scan_data(scan_id), session)
    comment = {
        "scan_id": scan_id,
        "author_name": session.name,
        "author_role": session.role,
        "comment_text": req.comment_text.strip(),
        "created_at": signing.utc_timestamp(),
    }
    comment["comment_id"] = len(_comments) + 1
    _comments.append(comment)
    import threading

    def _persist_comment() -> None:
        conn = _safe_connect()
        if not conn:
            return
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO public.scan_comments (scan_id, author_name, author_role, comment_text) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s)",
                    (scan_id, comment["author_name"], comment["author_role"], comment["comment_text"]),
                )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            conn.rollback()
            print(f"[collab_router] comment persistence skipped: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()

    threading.Thread(target=_persist_comment, daemon=True, name="scan-comment-persist").start()
    return {"status": "created", "comment": comment}


@router.get("/api/scans/{scan_id}/activity")
def scan_activity(scan_id: str, since: Optional[datetime] = Query(default=None), session: SessionUser = Depends(get_current_session)):
    scan = _scan_data(scan_id)
    _check_scan_workspace(scan, session)
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    conn = _safe_connect()
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT comment_id, author_name, author_role, comment_text, created_at FROM public.scan_comments WHERE scan_id = %s ORDER BY created_at ASC", (scan_id,))
                for row in cur.fetchall():
                    items.append({"type": "comment", "comment_id": row[0], "author_name": row[1], "author_role": row[2], "comment_text": row[3], "created_at": _iso(row[4])})
                cur.execute("SELECT version_no, text_snapshot, score, actor_name, created_at FROM public.scan_versions WHERE scan_id = %s ORDER BY version_no ASC", (scan_id,))
                for row in cur.fetchall():
                    items.append({"type": "version", "version_no": int(row[0]), "text_snapshot": row[1], "score": int(row[2]), "actor_name": row[3], "created_at": _iso(row[4])})
        except Exception as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=f"Could not load scan activity: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()
    else:
        items.extend({"type": "comment", **c} for c in _comments if c["scan_id"] == scan_id)
        items.extend({"type": "version", **v} for v in _memory_versions.get(scan_id, []))

    # Background writes may still be in flight when the UI polls activity.
    # Merge pending in-memory records with DB results while preserving repeated
    # identical comments as separate entries.
    db_comment_counts = Counter(
        (item.get("author_name"), item.get("author_role"), item.get("comment_text"))
        for item in items if item.get("type") == "comment"
    )
    for comment in _comments:
        if comment.get("scan_id") != scan_id:
            continue
        key = (comment.get("author_name"), comment.get("author_role"), comment.get("comment_text"))
        if db_comment_counts[key]:
            db_comment_counts[key] -= 1
        else:
            items.append({"type": "comment", **comment})

    db_version_numbers = {
        int(item["version_no"]) for item in items
        if item.get("type") == "version" and item.get("version_no") is not None
    }
    for version in _memory_versions.get(scan_id, []):
        if int(version["version_no"]) not in db_version_numbers:
            items.append({"type": "version", **version})
            db_version_numbers.add(int(version["version_no"]))
    items.extend({"type": "approval", **a} for a in _approval_rows(scan_id))
    if since:
        since_utc = since.astimezone(timezone.utc)
        items = [i for i in items if datetime.fromisoformat(_iso(i.get("created_at"))).astimezone(timezone.utc) > since_utc]
    items.sort(key=lambda i: i.get("created_at", ""))
    return {"scan_id": scan_id, "activity": items, "reaudit": scan.get("reaudit") or {"needed": False, "note": None}}


def _unresolved_high_issues(scan: Dict[str, Any], text: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    normalized_text = " ".join((text or "").casefold().split())
    blockers = []
    for issue in scan.get("issues") or []:
        if str(issue.get("severity", "")).casefold() != "high":
            continue
        flagged = " ".join(str(issue.get("flagged_text") or "").casefold().split())
        if flagged and flagged in normalized_text:
            blockers.append(issue)
    return blockers


@router.post("/api/scans/{scan_id}/approve")
def approve_scan(
    scan_id: str,
    req: ApprovalRequest = ApprovalRequest(),
    session: SessionUser = Depends(require_role("Reviewer", "Admin")),
):
    global _approvals_loaded
    scan = _scan_data(scan_id)
    _check_scan_workspace(scan, session)
    if scan.get("status") == "needs_reaudit" or (scan.get("reaudit") or {}).get("needed"):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="This scan needs a new audit before approval.")
    version = _latest_version(scan_id, scan, req.version_no)
    latest = _latest_version(scan_id, scan, None)
    if version["version_no"] != latest["version_no"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Only the latest scan version can be approved.")
    blockers = _unresolved_high_issues(scan, version["text_snapshot"])
    if blockers:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
            detail={
                "message": "Resolve all High severity issues before approving this scan.",
                "unresolved_high_issues": [i.get("id") for i in blockers],
            },
        )
    text_sha = hashlib.sha256(version["text_snapshot"].encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    created_at = signing.utc_timestamp()
    prev_hash = _approvals[-1]["entry_hash"] if _approvals else None
    entry_hash = signing.approval_entry_hash(
        scan_id=scan_id, version_no=version["version_no"], text_sha256=text_sha,
        score=version["score"], reviewer=session.name, role=session.role,
        prev_hash=prev_hash, created_at=created_at,
    )
    approval = {
        "scan_id": scan_id, "version_no": version["version_no"], "text_sha256": text_sha,
        "score": version["score"], "reviewer": session.name, "role": session.role,
        "prev_hash": prev_hash, "entry_hash": entry_hash,
        "signature": signing.sign_entry_hash(entry_hash), "created_at": created_at,
        "approval_id": max((int(a.get("approval_id") or 0) for a in _approvals), default=0) + 1,
    }
    _approvals.append(approval)
    _approvals_loaded = True
    approved_scan = {**scan, "status": "approved", "readiness_score": version["score"]}
    _sync_mem_scan(scan_id, approved_scan)

    import threading

    def _persist_approval() -> None:
        conn = _safe_connect()
        if not conn:
            return
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(731204918)")
                cur.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO public.approvals (scan_id, version_no, text_sha256, score, reviewer, role, prev_hash, entry_hash, signature, created_at) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)",
                    (scan_id, version["version_no"], text_sha, version["score"], session.name, session.role, prev_hash, entry_hash, approval["signature"], created_at),
                )
                cur.execute("UPDATE public.scan_documents SET result_json = %s::jsonb WHERE scan_id = %s", (json.dumps(approved_scan), scan_id))
                cur.execute("UPDATE public.scans SET status = 'approved', readiness_score = %s, payload = %s::jsonb WHERE scan_id = %s", (version["score"], json.dumps(approved_scan), scan_id))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            conn.rollback()
            print(f"[collab_router] approval persistence skipped: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()

    threading.Thread(target=_persist_approval, daemon=True, name="scan-approval-persist").start()
    return {"status": "approved", "approval": approval, "public_key": signing.public_key_b64()}


@router.get("/api/verify/{scan_id}")
def verify_scan_approvals(scan_id: str):
    scan = _scan_data(scan_id)
    all_rows = _approval_rows()
    if not all_rows:
        return {"scan_id": scan_id, "valid": False, "approvals": [], "reason": "No signed approval found."}
    expected_prev: Optional[str] = None
    versions_by_no: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {
        int(v["version_no"]): dict(v) for v in _memory_versions.get(scan_id, [])
    }
    conn = _safe_connect() if not versions_by_no else None
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "SELECT version_no, text_snapshot, score FROM public.scan_versions WHERE scan_id = %s ORDER BY version_no ASC",
                    (scan_id,),
                )
                versions_by_no = {
                    int(r[0]): {"version_no": int(r[0]), "text_snapshot": r[1] or "", "score": int(r[2] or 0)}
                    for r in cur.fetchall()
                }
        except Exception:
            versions_by_no = {}
        finally:
            conn.close()
    if not versions_by_no:
        versions_by_no = {int(v["version_no"]): dict(v) for v in _memory_versions.get(scan_id, [])}
    if not versions_by_no:
        versions_by_no[1] = {"version_no": 1, "text_snapshot": scan.get("redacted_text") or "", "score": int(scan.get("readiness_score") or 0)}
    chain_valid = True
    verified = []
    target_rows = []
    for row in all_rows:
        calculated = signing.approval_entry_hash(
            scan_id=row["scan_id"], version_no=row["version_no"], text_sha256=row["text_sha256"],
            score=row["score"], reviewer=row["reviewer"], role=row["role"],
            prev_hash=row["prev_hash"], created_at=row["created_at"],
        )
        row_valid = row["prev_hash"] == expected_prev and calculated == row["entry_hash"] and signing.verify_entry_hash(row["entry_hash"], row["signature"])
        chain_valid = chain_valid and row_valid
        expected_prev = row["entry_hash"]
        if row["scan_id"] == scan_id:
            try:
                signed_version = versions_by_no.get(int(row["version_no"]))
                if not signed_version:
                    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Scan version not found.")
                content_matches = hashlib.sha256(signed_version["text_snapshot"].encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() == row["text_sha256"]
            except HTTPException:
                content_matches = False
            target_rows.append({**row, "valid": row_valid and content_matches, "content_matches": content_matches})
    verified = target_rows
    latest_version = _latest_version(scan_id, scan, None)
    latest_approval = verified[-1] if verified else None
    tampered = bool(latest_approval and (
        not latest_approval["content_matches"]
        or latest_approval["version_no"] != latest_version["version_no"]
    ))
    valid = bool(verified) and chain_valid and all(a["valid"] for a in verified) and not tampered
    return {
        "scan_id": scan_id,
        "valid": valid,
        "tampered": tampered,
        "chain_valid": chain_valid,
        "approvals": verified,
        "certificate": latest_approval,
        "public_key": signing.public_key_b64(),
    }


@router.get("/api/public-key")
def get_public_key():
    return {"algorithm": "Ed25519", "encoding": "base64-raw-32-byte", "public_key": signing.public_key_b64()}


========================================================================================
FILE: db.py
========================================================================================
"""Database layer. Every function works even if Neon is down or not configured:
rules fall back to seed_db.py, events fall back to memory. A scan never crashes because of the DB."""
import os
import threading
import time
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from seed_db import DEMO_FLAG_EVENTS, FACTS_DATA, RULES_DATA

try:
    import psycopg2
    from psycopg2.extras import Json, RealDictCursor
    from psycopg2.extensions import connection as _PgConnection, STATUS_BEGIN, TRANSACTION_STATUS_INERROR
except Exception:  # psycopg2 missing -> run in local mode
    psycopg2 = None
    _PgConnection = object
    STATUS_BEGIN = TRANSACTION_STATUS_INERROR = -1

OK_TTL = 300     # re-read rules from Neon every 5 minutes
FAIL_TTL = 120   # if Neon failed, don't retry for 2 minutes (avoids slow requests)

_cache = {"at": 0.0, "ttl": 0, "rules": list(RULES_DATA), "facts": list(FACTS_DATA), "source": "local-fallback", "flag_events": None, "flag_events_loaded": False}
_memory_events: List[Dict] = [
    {"scan_id": s, "rule_id": r, "action": a, "reason": why} for s, r, a, why in DEMO_FLAG_EVENTS
]

for _rule in RULES_DATA:
    _rule.setdefault("workspace_id", "default")


_db_lock = threading.RLock()
_lease_state = threading.local()
_sticky_conn: Optional["_StickyConnection"] = None
_sticky_dsn: Optional[str] = None


def _is_connection_error(exc: Exception) -> bool:
    return bool(
        psycopg2
        and isinstance(exc, (psycopg2.OperationalError, psycopg2.InterfaceError))
    )


def reset_connection(conn=None) -> None:
    """Discard a dead sticky socket so the next request can reconnect."""
    global _sticky_conn, _sticky_dsn
    with _db_lock:
        if conn is None or conn is _sticky_conn:
            if _sticky_conn is not None:
                _sticky_conn.force_close()
            _sticky_conn = None
            _sticky_dsn = None


class _LockedCursor:
    def __init__(self, cursor, connection):
        self._cursor = cursor
        self._connection = connection
        self._released = False

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        return getattr(self._cursor, name)

    def _call(self, method, *args, **kwargs):
        try:
            return getattr(self._cursor, method)(*args, **kwargs)
        except Exception as exc:
            if _is_connection_error(exc):
                reset_connection(self._connection)
            raise

    def execute(self, *args, **kwargs):
        return self._call("execute", *args, **kwargs)

    def executemany(self, *args, **kwargs):
        return self._call("executemany", *args, **kwargs)

    def fetchone(self):
        return self._call("fetchone")

    def fetchmany(self, *args, **kwargs):
        return self._call("fetchmany", *args, **kwargs)

    def fetchall(self):
        return self._call("fetchall")

    def _release(self):
        if not self._released:
            self._released = True
            _db_lock.release()

    def close(self):
        try:
            return self._cursor.close()
        finally:
            self._release()

    def __enter__(self):
        self._cursor.__enter__()
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
        try:
            return self._cursor.__exit__(exc_type, exc, tb)
        finally:
            self._release()


class _StickyConnection(_PgConnection):
    """One persistent database socket, with serialized cursor use."""
    def cursor(self, *args, **kwargs):
        _db_lock.acquire()
        try:
            return _LockedCursor(super().cursor(*args, **kwargs), self)
        except Exception:
            _db_lock.release()
            raise

    def commit(self):
        with _db_lock:
            try:
                return super().commit()
            except Exception as exc:
                if _is_connection_error(exc):
                    reset_connection(self)
                raise

    def rollback(self):
        with _db_lock:
            try:
                return super().rollback()
            except Exception as exc:
                if _is_connection_error(exc):
                    reset_connection(self)
                raise

    def close(self):
        with _db_lock:
            try:
                if not self.closed and (
                    self.status == STATUS_BEGIN
                    or self.get_transaction_status() == TRANSACTION_STATUS_INERROR
                ):
                    super().rollback()
            except Exception:
                pass
            leases = getattr(_lease_state, "count", 0)
            if leases:
                _lease_state.count = leases - 1
                _db_lock.release()

    def force_close(self):
        try:
            super().close()
        except Exception:
            pass


def _connect():
    global _sticky_conn, _sticky_dsn
    url = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL", "").strip()
    if not psycopg2 or not url or "ep-xyz" in url or "user:password" in url:
        return None
    _db_lock.acquire()
    try:
        if _sticky_conn is not None and _sticky_dsn != url:
            _sticky_conn.force_close()
            _sticky_conn = None
        if _sticky_conn is not None and not _sticky_conn.closed:
            _lease_state.count = getattr(_lease_state, "count", 0) + 1
            return _sticky_conn
        _sticky_conn = psycopg2.connect(
            url,
            connect_timeout=5,
            connection_factory=_StickyConnection,
            keepalives=1,
            keepalives_idle=30,
            keepalives_interval=10,
            keepalives_count=5,
        )
        _sticky_dsn = url
        _lease_state.count = getattr(_lease_state, "count", 0) + 1
        return _sticky_conn
    except Exception:
        _db_lock.release()
        raise


# Backwards-compatible import used by seed scripts and downstream tests.
get_db_connection = _connect


def ping() -> bool:
    """Wakes Neon from its idle sleep. Used by /health."""
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = _connect()
        if not conn:
            return False
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT 1;")
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[db] ping failed: {e}")
        return False
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()


def get_vault(force: bool = False) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[Dict], str]:
    """Returns (rules, facts, source). source is 'neon' or 'local-fallback'."""
    now = time.time()
    if not force and now - _cache["at"] < _cache["ttl"]:
        return _cache["rules"], _cache["facts"], _cache["source"]
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = _connect()
        if not conn:
            raise RuntimeError("DATABASE_URL not configured")
        with conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor) as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM brand_rules ORDER BY rule_id;")
            rules = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM facts ORDER BY fact_id;")
            facts = [dict(f) for f in cur.fetchall()]
            try:
                cur.execute("SAVEPOINT flag_events_prefetch")
                cur.execute("SELECT scan_id, rule_id, action, reason FROM flag_events;")
                flag_events = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
                cur.execute("RELEASE SAVEPOINT flag_events_prefetch")
            except Exception:
                cur.execute("ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT flag_events_prefetch")
                cur.execute("RELEASE SAVEPOINT flag_events_prefetch")
                flag_events = []
            try:
                cur.execute("SAVEPOINT rule_version_prefetch")
                cur.execute("SELECT rule_id, version_no, old_rule_text, old_pattern, changed_by, created_at FROM public.rule_versions ORDER BY version_no ASC, id ASC;")
                rule_version_rows = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
                max_version = max((int(r["version_no"] or 1) for r in rule_version_rows), default=1)
                cur.execute("RELEASE SAVEPOINT rule_version_prefetch")
            except Exception:
                cur.execute("ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT rule_version_prefetch")
                cur.execute("RELEASE SAVEPOINT rule_version_prefetch")
                max_version = 1
                rule_version_rows = []
            try:
                cur.execute("SAVEPOINT approvals_prefetch")
                cur.execute("SELECT approval_id, scan_id, version_no, text_sha256, score, reviewer, role, prev_hash, entry_hash, signature, created_at FROM public.approvals ORDER BY created_at ASC, approval_id ASC;")
                approval_rows = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
                cur.execute("RELEASE SAVEPOINT approvals_prefetch")
            except Exception:
                cur.execute("ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT approvals_prefetch")
                cur.execute("RELEASE SAVEPOINT approvals_prefetch")
                approval_rows = []
        conn.close()
        if not rules:
            raise RuntimeError("brand_rules table is empty (run python seed_db.py)")
        for r in rules:
            r.setdefault("allowed_numbers", None)
            r["applies_to"] = r.get("applies_to") or "text"
        _cache.update(at=now, ttl=OK_TTL, rules=rules, facts=facts, source="neon", flag_events=flag_events, flag_events_loaded=True)
        try:
            import collab_router
            if not collab_router._approvals_loaded:
                collab_router._approvals[:] = [
                    {**r, "approval_id": int(r["approval_id"]), "version_no": int(r["version_no"]),
                     "score": int(r["score"]), "created_at": collab_router._iso(r["created_at"])}
                    for r in approval_rows
                ]
                collab_router._approvals_loaded = True
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            import rules_router
            histories: Dict[int, List[Dict]] = {}
            for row in rule_version_rows:
                rule_id = int(row["rule_id"])
                histories.setdefault(rule_id, []).append({
                    "version_no": int(row["version_no"]),
                    "rule_text": row["old_rule_text"],
                    "regex_pattern": row["old_pattern"],
                    "changed_by": row["changed_by"],
                    "created_at": str(row["created_at"]),
                })
            for rule_id, history in histories.items():
                if rule_id not in rules_router._history_loaded and rule_id not in rules_router._memory_history:
                    rules_router._memory_history[rule_id] = history
                    rules_router._history_loaded.add(rule_id)
            for rule in rules:
                rule_id = int(rule.get("rule_id") or 0)
                if rule_id and rule_id not in rules_router._history_loaded:
                    rules_router._memory_history.setdefault(rule_id, [])
                    rules_router._history_loaded.add(rule_id)
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            from rule_version import set_rules_version
            set_rules_version(max_version)
        except Exception:
            pass
    except Exception as e:
        if conn:
            conn.close()
        print(f"[db] using local rules ({e})")
        _cache.update(at=now, ttl=FAIL_TTL, rules=list(RULES_DATA), facts=list(FACTS_DATA), source="local-fallback", flag_events=list(_memory_events), flag_events_loaded=True)
    return _cache["rules"], _cache["facts"], _cache["source"]


def get_workspace_vault(workspace_id: str = "default", force: bool = False) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[Dict], str]:
    """Return baseline rules plus rules scoped to the requested workspace."""
    workspace = (workspace_id or "default").strip() or "default"
    rules, facts, source = get_vault(force=force)
    scoped_rules = [
        dict(rule) for rule in rules
        if rule.get("workspace_id", "default") == "default"
        or (workspace != "default" and rule.get("workspace_id") == workspace)
    ]
    return scoped_rules, facts, source


def rules_by_id() -> Dict[int, Dict]:
    return {r["rule_id"]: r for r in get_vault()[0]}


def save_scan(scan_id: str, score: int, status: str, input_type: str, payload: dict) -> None:
    """Best-effort audit log. Called after the response is sent, so it never slows the user."""
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = _connect()
        if not conn:
            return
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """INSERT INTO scans (scan_id, readiness_score, status, input_type, payload)
                   VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s) ON CONFLICT (scan_id) DO NOTHING;""",
                (scan_id, score, status, input_type, Json(payload)),
            )
        conn.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[db] save_scan failed: {e}")
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()


def log_flag_event(scan_id: str, rule_id: int, action: str, reason: str) -> str:
    """Returns where it was stored: 'neon' or 'memory'."""
    event = {"scan_id": scan_id, "rule_id": rule_id, "action": action, "reason": reason or ""}
    _memory_events.append(event)
    if _cache.get("flag_events") is None:
        _cache["flag_events"] = []
    _cache["flag_events"].append(event)
    _cache["flag_events_loaded"] = True

    def _persist() -> None:
        conn = None
        try:
            conn = _connect()
            if not conn:
                return
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO flag_events (scan_id, rule_id, action, reason) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s);",
                    (scan_id, rule_id, action, reason or ""),
                )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[db] log_flag_event failed, keeping in memory: {exc}")
        finally:
            if conn:
                conn.close()

    threading.Thread(target=_persist, daemon=True, name="flag-event-persist").start()
    return "memory"


def get_flag_events() -> List[Dict]:
    if _cache.get("flag_events_loaded"):
        return list(_cache.get("flag_events") or _memory_events)
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = _connect()
        if conn:
            with conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor) as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT scan_id, rule_id, action, reason FROM flag_events;")
                rows = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
            return rows
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[db] get_flag_events failed, using memory: {e}")
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()
    return list(_memory_events)


========================================================================================
FILE: engine.py
