"""Tier 1 (no AI): PII redaction, the regex rule engine, channel-specific governance rules,
no-op fix filtering, and highlight-position helpers."""
import re
from functools import lru_cache
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from schemas import WEIGHTS, IssueItem, normalize_channel

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- PII redaction
# Every match is replaced by the SAME number of "X" so start/end positions never shift.
PII_PATTERNS = [
    r"[A-Za-z0-9_.+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9.-]+",                              # emails
    r"(?<!\d)(?:\d[ -]?){13,19}(?!\d)",                                              # card-like numbers
    r"(?<!\w)\+\d{1,3}[\s.-]?\d{2,5}[\s.-]?\d{3,5}(?:[\s.-]?\d{2,5})?(?!\d)",       # +CC phone numbers
    r"(?<!\d)[6-9]\d{4}[\s-]?\d{5}(?!\d)",                                           # Indian 10-digit mobiles
]

# Channel-specific deterministic rules (applies when channel != "general")
CHANNEL_REGEX_RULES: Dict[str, List[Dict]] = {
    "linkedin": [
        {
            "rule_id": 1001,
            "violation_type": "Tone",
            "severity": "Low",
            "rule_cited": "Channel Rule (LinkedIn): Professional CTA Standard",
            "source_ref": "Channel Playbook 2026 — LinkedIn B2B Voice",
            "rule_text": "Avoid casual social slang ('DM me', 'link in bio', 'smash that like') on LinkedIn.",
            "regex_pattern": r"(?i)\b(?:dm\s+me|link\s+in\s+bio|smash\s+that\s+like(?:\s+button)?|drop\s+a\s+fire\s+emoji)\b",
            "default_fix": "connect with our team",
        },
    ],
    "instagram": [
        {
            "rule_id": 1002,
            "violation_type": "Accessibility",
            "severity": "Low",
            "rule_cited": "Channel Rule (Instagram): Non-Clickable URL in Caption",
            "source_ref": "Channel Playbook 2026 — Instagram Formatting",
            "rule_text": "Raw URLs in Instagram captions are not clickable. Direct Creators to the link in bio instead.",
            "regex_pattern": r"https?://[^\s)>\]]+",
            "default_fix": "the link in our bio",
        },
    ],
    "billboard": [
        {
            "rule_id": 1003,
            "violation_type": "Accessibility",
            "severity": "Medium",
            "rule_cited": "Channel Rule (Billboard): No Web URLs or Click Prompts on OOH Assets",
            "source_ref": "Channel Playbook 2026 — Out-of-Home (Billboard) Safety",
            "rule_text": "Motorists cannot click links or type long URLs while driving. Use a short brand name search prompt instead.",
            "regex_pattern": r"(?i)(?:https?://[^\s)>\]]+|www\.[^\s)>\]]+|\b(?:click|tap)\s+(?:here|now|below)\b)",
            "default_fix": "Search ClaimLoom",
        },
    ],
    "email": [
        {
            "rule_id": 1004,
            "violation_type": "Tone",
            "severity": "Medium",
            "rule_cited": "Channel Rule (Email): Deliverability & Spam-Trigger Filter",
            "source_ref": "Channel Playbook 2026 — Email Compliance",
            "rule_text": "Avoid high-pressure spam trigger phrases ('Act now', '100% free', 'Urgent action required', 'Click below immediately') that route emails to spam folders.",
            "regex_pattern": r"(?i)\b(?:act\s+now|urgent\s+action\s+required|100%\s+free|free\s+money|no\s+obligation|click\s+below\s+immediately|once\s+in\s+a\s+lifetime)\b",
            "default_fix": "explore the details",
        },
        {
            "rule_id": 1005,
            "violation_type": "Tone",
            "severity": "Low",
            "rule_cited": "Channel Rule (Email): No ALL-CAPS Shouting Phrases",
            "source_ref": "Channel Playbook 2026 — Email Compliance",
            "rule_text": "Avoid 3+ consecutive ALL-CAPS words in marketing emails, which trigger spam filters and hurt readability.",
            "regex_pattern": r"\b[A-Z]{4,}(?:\s+[A-Z]{3,}){2,}\b",
            "default_fix": None,  # dynamically title/sentence-cased in _pick_fix
        },
    ],
}


def redact_pii_same_length(text: str) -> Tuple[str, int]:
    count = 0

    def mask(match):
        nonlocal count
        count += 1
        return "X" * len(match.group(0))

    for pattern in PII_PATTERNS:
        text = re.sub(pattern, mask, text)
    return text, count


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
@lru_cache(maxsize=256)
def compile_pattern(pattern: str):
    return re.compile(pattern)


def is_noop_fix(flagged_text: str, suggested_fix: str) -> bool:
    """Returns True if the suggested fix is empty or identical to the flagged text
    (prevents the 'ClaimLoom' -> 'ClaimLoom' self-flagging bug)."""
    f_clean = (flagged_text or "").strip()
    s_clean = (suggested_fix or "").strip()
    if not f_clean or not s_clean:
        return True
    return f_clean == s_clean


def to_utf16(text: str, index: int) -> int:
    """Python counts emoji as 1 character, JavaScript counts them as 2. Convert so highlights match."""
    if index is None or index < 0:
        return -1
    return len(text[:index].encode("utf-16-le")) // 2


def overlaps(a_start: int, a_end: int, spans: List[Tuple[int, int]]) -> bool:
    if a_start < 0 or a_end < 0:
        return False
    return any(s >= 0 and e >= 0 and a_start < e and a_end > s for s, e in spans)


def locate_phrase(draft: str, phrase: str, taken: List[Tuple[int, int]]) -> Tuple[int, int]:
    """Finds `phrase` in the ORIGINAL draft. Ignores case/extra spaces, and skips
    occurrences that are already flagged, so repeated phrases each get their own highlight."""
    words = (phrase or "").split()
    if not words:
        return -1, -1
    pattern = r"\s+".join(map(re.escape, words))
    for m in re.finditer(pattern, draft, re.IGNORECASE):
        if not overlaps(m.start(), m.end(), taken):
            return m.start(), m.end()
    return -1, -1


def _pick_fix(rule: Dict, matched: str) -> str:
    fix = rule.get("default_fix")
    if not fix:
        # Fallback for ALL-CAPS shouting rule -> convert to capitalized sentence case
        if matched.isupper() and len(matched) > 3:
            return matched.capitalize()
        return matched
    if "|" in fix:  # "Creator|Creators" -> singular|plural
        singular, plural = fix.split("|", 1)
        fix = plural if matched.lower().endswith("s") else singular
    return fix


def _allowed_numbers(rule: Dict) -> set:
    raw = rule.get("allowed_numbers")
    if not raw:
        return set()
    return {float(x) for x in str(raw).split(",") if x.strip()}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Channel structural checks
def run_channel_rules(text: str, channel: str = "general") -> List[IssueItem]:
    """Runs deterministic channel-specific rules for linkedin, instagram, billboard, and email."""
    ch = normalize_channel(channel)
    if ch == "general":
        return []

    found: List[IssueItem] = []

    # 1. Regex-based channel rules
    for rule in CHANNEL_REGEX_RULES.get(ch, []):
        rx = compile_pattern(rule["regex_pattern"])
        for m in rx.finditer(text):
            start, end = m.span()
            matched = text[start:end]
            fix = _pick_fix(rule, matched)
            if is_noop_fix(matched, fix):
                continue
            found.append(
                IssueItem(
                    id="",
                    severity=rule["severity"],
                    violation_type=rule["violation_type"],
                    flagged_text=matched,
                    start=start,
                    end=end,
                    rule_id=rule["rule_id"],
                    rule_cited=rule["rule_cited"],
                    source_ref=rule["source_ref"],
                    explanation=f"Violates {rule['rule_cited']}. {rule['rule_text']}",
                    suggested_fix=fix,
                    confidence=1.0,
                    detected_by="rule_engine",
                    claim_status=None,
                    channel=ch,
                )
            )

    # 2. Structural channel checks (Billboard word cap & LinkedIn hashtag cap)
    if ch == "billboard":
        word_matches = list(re.finditer(r"\S+", text))
        if len(word_matches) > 12:
            # Flag the overflow span starting from word 13 to the end of the text
            overflow_start = word_matches[12].start()
            overflow_end = word_matches[-1].end()
            flagged_overflow = text[overflow_start:overflow_end]
            concise_fix = " ".join(m.group(0) for m in word_matches[:10]).rstrip(".,;:") + "."
            if not is_noop_fix(flagged_overflow, concise_fix):
                found.append(
                    IssueItem(
                        id="",
                        severity="Medium",
                        violation_type="Accessibility",
                        flagged_text=flagged_overflow,
                        start=overflow_start,
                        end=overflow_end,
                        rule_id=1006,
                        rule_cited="Channel Rule (Billboard): Maximum 12-Word Readability Limit",
                        source_ref="Channel Playbook 2026 — Out-of-Home (Billboard) Legibility",
                        explanation=(
                            f"Billboard copy has {len(word_matches)} words (limit is 12 words for 3-second highway legibility). "
                            "Trim the trailing copy so motorists can read it at a glance."
                        ),
                        suggested_fix="[Remove excess words to stay under 12 words]",
                        confidence=1.0,
                        detected_by="rule_engine",
                        claim_status=None,
                        channel=ch,
                    )
                )

    elif ch == "linkedin":
        hashtags = list(re.finditer(r"(?<!\w)#[A-Za-z0-9_]+", text))
        if len(hashtags) > 5:
            overflow_start = hashtags[5].start()
            overflow_end = hashtags[-1].end()
            flagged_tags = text[overflow_start:overflow_end]
            keep_three = " ".join(h.group(0) for h in hashtags[:3])
            if not is_noop_fix(flagged_tags, keep_three):
                found.append(
                    IssueItem(
                        id="",
                        severity="Low",
                        violation_type="Tone",
                        flagged_text=flagged_tags,
                        start=overflow_start,
                        end=overflow_end,
                        rule_id=1007,
                        rule_cited="Channel Rule (LinkedIn): Maximum 5 Hashtags",
                        source_ref="Channel Playbook 2026 — LinkedIn B2B Voice",
                        explanation=(
                            f"Post contains {len(hashtags)} hashtags (maximum 5 allowed on LinkedIn). "
                            "Excess hashtags reduce feed distribution and look spammy."
                        ),
                        suggested_fix=keep_three,
                        confidence=1.0,
                        detected_by="rule_engine",
                        claim_status=None,
                        channel=ch,
                    )
                )

        # LinkedIn copy should avoid shouty capitalization and stacked punctuation.
        for match in re.finditer(r"!{2,}", text):
            flagged = match.group(0)
            if not is_noop_fix(flagged, "!"):
                found.append(IssueItem(
                    id="", severity="Low", violation_type="Tone", flagged_text=flagged,
                    start=match.start(), end=match.end(), rule_id=1008,
                    rule_cited="Channel Rule (LinkedIn): Professional Punctuation",
                    source_ref="Channel Playbook 2026 — LinkedIn B2B Voice",
                    explanation="Use at most one exclamation mark in professional LinkedIn copy.",
                    suggested_fix="!", confidence=1.0, detected_by="rule_engine", channel=ch,
                ))
        for match in re.finditer(r"\b[A-Z]{6,}\b", text):
            flagged = match.group(0)
            fix = flagged.capitalize()
            if not is_noop_fix(flagged, fix):
                found.append(IssueItem(
                    id="", severity="Low", violation_type="Tone", flagged_text=flagged,
                    start=match.start(), end=match.end(), rule_id=1009,
                    rule_cited="Channel Rule (LinkedIn): Avoid All-Caps Copy",
                    source_ref="Channel Playbook 2026 — LinkedIn B2B Voice",
                    explanation="Avoid all-caps words longer than five letters; they read as shouting in professional posts.",
                    suggested_fix=fix, confidence=1.0, detected_by="rule_engine", channel=ch,
                ))

    elif ch == "instagram":
        words = list(re.finditer(r"\S+", text))
        if len(words) > 150 and "\n" not in text:
            flagged = text.strip()
            if flagged and not is_noop_fix(flagged, "Break copy into shorter paragraphs"):
                found.append(IssueItem(
                    id="", severity="Low", violation_type="Accessibility", flagged_text=flagged,
                    start=-1, end=-1, rule_id=1010,
                    rule_cited="Channel Rule (Instagram): Caption Readability",
                    source_ref="Channel Playbook 2026 — Instagram Formatting",
                    explanation=f"This {len(words)}-word caption has no line breaks. Split long copy into short, scannable paragraphs.",
                    suggested_fix="Break copy into shorter paragraphs", confidence=1.0,
                    detected_by="rule_engine", channel=ch,
                ))

    return found


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Tier 1 engine
def run_rule_engine(
    text: str,
    rules: List[Dict],
    scope: str = "text",
    channel: str = "general",
) -> List[IssueItem]:
    """Exact regex checks + channel rules. Zero hallucination, exact positions.
    Drops any candidate where flagged_text.strip() == suggested_fix.strip()."""
    ch = normalize_channel(channel)
    found: List[IssueItem] = []

    for rule in rules:
        if rule.get("applies_to", "text") not in (scope, "both"):
            continue
        # If a database rule specifies a channel column, honor it
        rule_channel = normalize_channel(rule.get("channel")) if rule.get("channel") else "general"
        if rule_channel != "general" and rule_channel != ch:
            continue

        pattern = rule.get("regex_pattern")
        if not pattern:
            continue
        try:
            rx = compile_pattern(pattern)
        except re.error:
            continue  # a bad pattern typed into the database must never crash a scan
        allowed = _allowed_numbers(rule)
        rule_id = int(rule.get("rule_id") or -1)
        matches = re.finditer(r"(?i)\b\d+(?:,\d{3})?\s*km\b", text) if rule_id == 2 else rx.finditer(text)
        for m in matches:
            # Battery range claims need range context. A speed such as 150 km/h
            # or a location 200 km away is not a product-range claim.
            if rule_id == 2:
                value_match = re.search(r"\d+(?:,\d{3})?", m.group(0))
                if not value_match or float(value_match.group(0).replace(",", "")) <= 110:
                    continue
                suffix = re.match(r"\s+range\b", text[m.end():], re.IGNORECASE)
                prefix = re.search(r"\brange\s+(?:(?:is|of)\s+)?$", text[max(0, m.start() - 24):m.start()], re.IGNORECASE)
                if suffix:
                    claim_start, claim_end = m.start(), m.end() + suffix.end()
                elif prefix:
                    claim_start = max(0, m.start() - 24) + prefix.start()
                    claim_end = m.end()
                else:
                    continue
            else:
                claim_start, claim_end = m.start(), m.end()
            if allowed and "num" in rx.groupindex and m.group("num"):
                try:
                    if float(m.group("num")) in allowed:
                        continue  # price/number is approved, no violation
                except ValueError:
                    pass
            if rule_id == 2:
                start, end = claim_start, claim_end
            elif "flag" in rx.groupindex and m.group("flag"):
                start, end = m.span("flag")
            else:
                start, end = m.span()
            matched = text[start:end]
            fix = _pick_fix(rule, matched)

            # Fix-First Guard: drop self-identical flags (e.g., "ClaimLoom" -> "ClaimLoom")
            if is_noop_fix(matched, fix):
                continue

            found.append(
                IssueItem(
                    id="",
                    severity=rule["severity"],
                    violation_type=rule["violation_type"],
                    flagged_text=matched,
                    start=start,
                    end=end,
                    rule_id=rule["rule_id"],
                    rule_cited=rule["rule_cited"],
                    source_ref=rule["source_ref"],
                    explanation=f"Violates {rule['rule_cited']}. {rule['rule_text']}",
                    suggested_fix=fix,
                    confidence=1.0,
                    detected_by="rule_engine",
                    claim_status=rule.get("claim_status"),
                    channel=ch,
                )
            )

    if scope in ("text", "both"):
        found.extend(run_channel_rules(text, channel=ch))

    return _drop_overlaps(found)


def _drop_overlaps(issues: List[IssueItem]) -> List[IssueItem]:
    """If two rules flag the same words, keep the more severe one."""
    issues.sort(key=lambda i: (i.start if i.start is not None else -1, -WEIGHTS.get(i.severity, 12)))
    kept: List[IssueItem] = []
    for iss in issues:
        s = iss.start if iss.start is not None else -1
        e = iss.end if iss.end is not None else -1
        if not overlaps(s, e, [(k.start or -1, k.end or -1) for k in kept]):
            kept.append(iss)
    return kept

"""Truth Vault endpoints backed by public.kb_chunks."""
import json
import re
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Body, HTTPException

import cache
import db
import pipeline
import rule_version
import seed_kb
from seed_kb import KB_CHUNKS

router = APIRouter()

_PRICE_RE = re.compile(r"\$\s*(\d+(?:\.\d{1,2})?)\s*(?:/|per\s+)?(?:month|mo)\b", re.I)


def _safe_connect():
    try:
        return db._connect()
    except Exception:
        return None


def _read_chunks(workspace_id: str = "default"):
    workspace = (workspace_id or "default").strip() or "default"
    conn = _safe_connect()
    if not conn:
        return KB_CHUNKS
    try:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            try:
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS doc_title TEXT;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default';")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS page_num INT DEFAULT 1;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS paragraph_num INT DEFAULT 1;")
                conn.commit()
            except Exception:
                conn.rollback()

            if workspace == "default":
                cur.execute("SELECT chunk_id,doc_title,page_num,paragraph_num,chunk_text,updated_at FROM public.kb_chunks WHERE workspace_id=%s ORDER BY chunk_id", (workspace,))
            else:
                cur.execute("SELECT chunk_id,doc_title,page_num,paragraph_num,chunk_text,updated_at FROM public.kb_chunks WHERE workspace_id IN ('default', %s) ORDER BY chunk_id", (workspace,))
            rows = cur.fetchall()
        return [{"chunk_id": r[0], "doc_title": r[1] or f"Spec #{r[0]}", "page_num": r[2] or 1, "paragraph_num": r[3] or 1, "chunk_text": r[4], "updated_at": str(r[5]) if r[5] else None} for r in rows] or KB_CHUNKS
    except Exception:
        return KB_CHUNKS
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass


@router.get("/api/kb/sources")
@router.get("/api/kb/chunks")
def get_kb_sources(workspace_id: str = "default"):
    """Return truth vault knowledge chunks and active KB version."""
    chunks = _read_chunks(workspace_id)
    kb_ver = pipeline.get_kb_version(workspace_id)
    return {"chunks": chunks, "kb_version": kb_ver}


def ensure_demo_scans_seeded():
    """Seed demo_scan_1 and demo_scan_2 into memory stores if they are missing.

    Called at lifespan startup and at the start of _update_chunk() for chunk 3
    so that clicking "Demo Price Change & Sync" on a fresh server always
    returns affected_count >= 2.
    """
    import main
    import pdf_engine
    import rag_verifier

    demo_scans = [
        ("demo_scan_1", "Our new Pro Plan is amazing at just $29/month!"),
        ("demo_scan_2", "Sign up today for $29/month to get the Pro Plan features."),
    ]

    for scan_id, text in demo_scans:
        if scan_id not in main._mem_scans:
            evidence = {
                "verdict": "Verified",
                "doc_title": "Approved Pricing Sheet Q4",
                "page": 1,
                "paragraph": 1,
                "excerpt": "ClaimLoom Pro Plan subscription price is $29/month or $290/year.",
                "similarity": 0.95,
                "chunk_id": 3,
            }
            payload = {
                "scan_id": scan_id,
                "readiness_score": 100,
                "status": "approved",
                "ai_status": "ok",
                "pillar_scores": {"tone": 100, "terminology": 100, "claims": 100, "accessibility": 100, "visual": 100},
                "ai_disclosure": "Pre-approved ClaimLoom demo scan.",
                "pii_redacted_count": 0,
                "issues": [],
                "input_type": "text",
                "redacted_text": text,
                "cached": False,
                "channel": "general",
                "workspace_id": "default",
                "kb_version": 1,
                "rules_version": 1,
                "page_count": 1,
                "verified_claims": [evidence],
                "reaudit": {"needed": False, "note": None},
            }
            main._mem_scans[scan_id] = payload
            pdf_engine._mem_scan_json[scan_id] = payload

    # Ensure evidence rows exist for chunk 3 for both demo scans
    existing_ids = {str(row.get("scan_id")) for row in rag_verifier._mem_evidence}
    for scan_id, text in demo_scans:
        if scan_id not in existing_ids:
            rag_verifier._mem_evidence.append({
                "scan_id": scan_id,
                "claim_text": text,
                "chunk_id": 3,
                "verdict": "Verified",
                "similarity": 0.95,
            })

    # Also persist to Neon if connected and demo_scan_1 not already there
    conn = _safe_connect()
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT 1 FROM public.scan_documents WHERE scan_id='demo_scan_1' LIMIT 1")
                already = cur.fetchone()
                if not already:
                    import main as _main
                    for scan_id, text in demo_scans:
                        payload = _main._mem_scans.get(scan_id, {})
                        cur.execute(
                            """INSERT INTO public.scan_documents
                               (scan_id, workspace_id, channel, redacted_text, result_json, reaudit_needed, reaudit_reason)
                               VALUES (%s, 'default', 'general', %s, %s::jsonb, FALSE, NULL)
                               ON CONFLICT (scan_id) DO NOTHING""",
                            (scan_id, text, json.dumps(payload)),
                        )
                        cur.execute(
                            """INSERT INTO public.claim_evidence (scan_id, claim_text, chunk_id, verdict, similarity)
                               VALUES (%s, %s, 3, 'Verified', 0.95)
                               ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING""",
                            (scan_id, text),
                        )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            try:
                conn.rollback()
            except Exception:
                pass
            print(f"[kb_router] ensure_demo_scans_seeded DB write skipped: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()


def _update_rule5_for_price(new_price: str):
    """Update Rule #5 in db._cache and optionally Neon for a new Pro Plan monthly price."""
    new_price = new_price.strip()
    for rule in db._cache.get("rules", []):
        if rule.get("rule_id") == 5:
            rule["allowed_numbers"] = new_price
            rule["default_fix"] = f"${new_price}/month"
            rule["rule_cited"] = f"Brand Rule 5: Pro Plan pricing is ${new_price}/month"
            rule["rule_text"] = f"The ClaimLoom Pro Plan costs ${new_price}/month (or ${int(float(new_price)) * 10}/year). Any other Pro price is outdated."
            break

    conn = _safe_connect()
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    """UPDATE public.brand_rules
                       SET allowed_numbers=%s, default_fix=%s, rule_cited=%s, rule_text=%s
                       WHERE rule_id=5""",
                    (
                        new_price,
                        f"${new_price}/month",
                        f"Brand Rule 5: Pro Plan pricing is ${new_price}/month",
                        f"The ClaimLoom Pro Plan costs ${new_price}/month (or ${int(float(new_price)) * 10}/year). Any other Pro price is outdated.",
                    ),
                )
                cur.execute(
                    "UPDATE public.facts SET fact_text=%s WHERE fact_id=103",
                    (f"ClaimLoom Pro Plan subscription price is ${new_price}/month or ${int(float(new_price)) * 10}/year.",),
                )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            try:
                conn.rollback()
            except Exception:
                pass
            print(f"[kb_router] Rule #5 Neon update skipped: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()

    rule_version.set_rules_version(rule_version.get_rules_version() + 1)


def _update_chunk(chunk_id: int, payload: dict):
    text = str(payload.get("chunk_text") or "").strip()
    if not text:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="chunk_text is required")

    # Fix 1: Seed demo scans if updating chunk 3 and evidence rows are missing
    if chunk_id == 3:
        import rag_verifier
        has_chunk3_evidence = any(
            row.get("chunk_id") == 3
            for row in rag_verifier._mem_evidence
        )
        if not has_chunk3_evidence:
            try:
                ensure_demo_scans_seeded()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(f"[kb_router] ensure_demo_scans_seeded failed: {exc}")

    conn = _safe_connect()
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                title = payload.get("doc_title")
                cur.execute("UPDATE public.kb_chunks SET chunk_text=%s, doc_title=COALESCE(%s,doc_title), updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE chunk_id=%s", (text, title, chunk_id))
                if cur.rowcount == 0:
                    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chunk not found")
                cur.execute("UPDATE public.kb_meta SET value=value+1 WHERE key='kb_version'")
            conn.commit()
        finally:
            conn.close()
    else:
        found = False
        for kc in seed_kb.KB_CHUNKS:
            if kc["chunk_id"] == chunk_id:
                kc["chunk_text"] = text
                if payload.get("doc_title"):
                    kc["doc_title"] = payload["doc_title"]
                found = True
                break
        if not found:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chunk not found")

    # Fix 1: Detect Pro Plan price change and update Rule #5
    doc_title = str(payload.get("doc_title") or "")
    is_pricing_chunk = (chunk_id == 3) or "pricing" in doc_title.lower() or "price" in doc_title.lower()
    if is_pricing_chunk:
        price_match = _PRICE_RE.search(text)
        if price_match:
            new_price = price_match.group(1).rstrip(".")
            try:
                _update_rule5_for_price(new_price)
                # Also update seed_kb.KB_CHUNKS in-memory for offline fallback
                for kc in seed_kb.KB_CHUNKS:
                    if kc["chunk_id"] == chunk_id:
                        kc["chunk_text"] = text
                        break
                # Force cache refresh so new rules take effect immediately
                db._cache["at"] = 0
            except Exception as exc:
                print(f"[kb_router] price sync update failed: {exc}")

    affected = pipeline.reaudit_changed([chunk_id])
    cache.invalidate_cache()
    return {"status": "updated", "chunk_id": chunk_id, "kb_version": pipeline.get_kb_version(), "affected_scans": affected, "affected_count": len(affected)}


@router.put("/api/kb/chunks/{chunk_id}")
def update_chunk(chunk_id: int, payload: dict = Body(...)):
    return _update_chunk(chunk_id, payload)


@router.put("/api/facts/{chunk_id}")
def update_fact(chunk_id: int, payload: dict = Body(...)):
    return _update_chunk(chunk_id, payload)


@router.post("/api/kb/sync")
def sync_kb():
    ids = [int(c["chunk_id"]) for c in _read_chunks()]
    affected = pipeline.reaudit_changed(ids)
    cache.invalidate_cache()
    return {"status": "synced", "kb_version": pipeline.get_kb_version(), "affected_scans": affected, "affected_count": len(affected)}


@router.post("/api/admin/reset-demo")
def reset_demo():
    from seed_db import FACTS_DATA, RULES_DATA
    from seed_kb import seed_kb as _seed_kb
    import rules_router
    import rag_verifier
    import main
    import pdf_engine

    demo_scans = [
        ("demo_scan_1", "Our new Pro Plan is amazing at just $29/month!"),
        ("demo_scan_2", "Sign up today for $29/month to get the Pro Plan features."),
    ]
    demo_evidence = []
    for scan_id, text in demo_scans:
        evidence = {
            "verdict": "Verified",
            "doc_title": "Approved Pricing Sheet Q4",
            "page": 1,
            "paragraph": 1,
            "excerpt": "ClaimLoom Pro Plan subscription price is $29/month or $290/year.",
            "similarity": 0.95,
            "chunk_id": 3,
        }
        payload = {
            "scan_id": scan_id,
            "readiness_score": 100,
            "status": "approved",
            "ai_status": "ok",
            "pillar_scores": {"tone": 100, "terminology": 100, "claims": 100, "accessibility": 100, "visual": 100},
            "ai_disclosure": "Pre-approved ClaimLoom demo scan.",
            "pii_redacted_count": 0,
            "issues": [],
            "input_type": "text",
            "redacted_text": text,
            "cached": False,
            "channel": "general",
            "workspace_id": "default",
            "kb_version": 1,
            "rules_version": 1,
            "page_count": 1,
            "verified_claims": [evidence],
            "reaudit": {"needed": False, "note": None},
        }
        main._mem_scans[scan_id] = payload
        pdf_engine._mem_scan_json[scan_id] = payload
        demo_evidence.append({"scan_id": scan_id, "claim_text": text, "chunk_id": 3, "verdict": "Verified", "similarity": 0.95})
    rag_verifier._mem_evidence[:] = [row for row in rag_verifier._mem_evidence if not str(row.get("scan_id", "")).startswith("demo_scan_")]
    rag_verifier._mem_evidence.extend(demo_evidence)

    # Restore Rule #5 to $29/month in-memory cache
    for rule in db._cache.get("rules", []):
        if rule.get("rule_id") == 5:
            rule["allowed_numbers"] = "29"
            rule["default_fix"] = "$29/month"
            rule["rule_cited"] = "Brand Rule 5: Pro Plan pricing is $29/month"
            rule["rule_text"] = "The ClaimLoom Pro Plan costs $29/month (or $290/year). Any other Pro price is outdated."
            break

    # Restore KB_CHUNKS chunk #3 in memory
    for kc in seed_kb.KB_CHUNKS:
        if kc["chunk_id"] == 3:
            kc["chunk_text"] = "ClaimLoom Pro Plan subscription price is $29/month or $290/year."
            break

    conn = _safe_connect()
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                # Self-healing schema migrations for legacy tables
                cur.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.kb_meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1);")
                cur.execute("INSERT INTO public.kb_meta (key, value) VALUES ('kb_version', 1) ON CONFLICT (key) DO NOTHING;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.brand_rules ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default';")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.brand_rules ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS version_no INT DEFAULT 1;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.brand_rules ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS allowed_numbers TEXT;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.brand_rules ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS applies_to VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'text';")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.facts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default';")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.scan_documents ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default';")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.scan_documents ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS channel VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'general';")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.scan_documents ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reaudit_needed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.scan_documents ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reaudit_reason TEXT;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.claim_evidence ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default';")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS doc_title TEXT;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS page_num INT DEFAULT 1;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS paragraph_num INT DEFAULT 1;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS chunk_text TEXT;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS content_hash TEXT;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS embedding JSONB;")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default';")
                cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;")

                cur.execute("DELETE FROM public.brand_rules")
                for r in RULES_DATA:
                    cur.execute("INSERT INTO public.brand_rules (rule_id,violation_type,severity,rule_cited,source_ref,rule_text,regex_pattern,default_fix,claim_status,allowed_numbers,applies_to,workspace_id) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s) ON CONFLICT (rule_id) DO UPDATE SET rule_text=EXCLUDED.rule_text", tuple(r.get(k) for k in ("rule_id", "violation_type", "severity", "rule_cited", "source_ref", "rule_text", "regex_pattern", "default_fix", "claim_status", "allowed_numbers", "applies_to", "workspace_id")))
                for f in FACTS_DATA:
                    cur.execute("INSERT INTO public.facts (fact_id,fact_text,source_ref,workspace_id) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,'default') ON CONFLICT (fact_id) DO UPDATE SET fact_text=EXCLUDED.fact_text,source_ref=EXCLUDED.source_ref", (f["fact_id"], f["fact_text"], f["source_ref"]))
                _seed_kb(conn)
                cur.execute("UPDATE public.kb_meta SET value=1 WHERE key='kb_version'")
                cur.execute("DELETE FROM public.scan_cache")
                for scan_id, text in demo_scans:
                    payload = main._mem_scans[scan_id]
                    cur.execute(
                        """INSERT INTO public.scan_documents
                           (scan_id, workspace_id, channel, redacted_text, result_json, reaudit_needed, reaudit_reason)
                           VALUES (%s, 'default', 'general', %s, %s::jsonb, FALSE, NULL)
                           ON CONFLICT (scan_id) DO UPDATE SET redacted_text=EXCLUDED.redacted_text,
                           result_json=EXCLUDED.result_json, reaudit_needed=FALSE, reaudit_reason=NULL""",
                        (scan_id, text, json.dumps(payload)),
                    )
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM public.claim_evidence WHERE scan_id=%s", (scan_id,))
                    cur.execute(
                        """INSERT INTO public.claim_evidence (scan_id, claim_text, chunk_id, verdict, similarity)
                           VALUES (%s, %s, 3, 'Verified', 0.95)""",
                        (scan_id, text),
                    )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            conn.rollback()
            print(f"[kb_router] reset_demo Neon DB write skipped or errored: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()

    db._cache["at"] = 0
    db.get_vault(force=True)
    rules_router._memory_history.clear()
    rules_router._history_loaded.clear()
    cache.invalidate_cache()
    return {"status": "reset", "rules_version": 1, "kb_version": 1}

from dotenv import load_dotenv

#Bounded in-memory fallback store for Collision Point 4 (?scan_id= reload)
# Ensures GET /api/scans/{scan_id} works even if Neon is asleep or unmigrated
_MEM_SCANS_LIMIT = 150
_mem_scans: "OrderedDict[str, Dict[str, Any]]" = OrderedDict()


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    db.get_vault(force=True)  # load the Brand Truth Vault from Neon once at startup (falls back to local)
    try:
        from kb_router import ensure_demo_scans_seeded
        ensure_demo_scans_seeded()
    except Exception as _lifespan_exc:
        print(f"[main] ensure_demo_scans_seeded skipped at startup: {_lifespan_exc}")
    yield


app = FastAPI(title="ClaimLoom Governance API v3", lifespan=lifespan)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Collision Point 2: Bulletproof CORS
raw_origins = os.getenv(
    "ALLOWED_ORIGINS",
    "http://localhost:3000,http://127.0.0.1:3000,https://oops-hackathon.vercel.app",
)
allowed_origins = [o.strip() for o in raw_origins.split(",") if o.strip() and "*" not in o]
if not allowed_origins:
    allowed_origins = [
        "http://localhost:3000",
        "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
        "https://oops-hackathon.vercel.app",
    ]

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=allowed_origins,
    allow_origin_regex=r"https://.*\.vercel\.app",  # Allows all Vercel preview & production links
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["GET", "POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"],
    allow_headers=["*"],  # Allows Authorization, X-Workspace-Id, Content-Type
    expose_headers=["Content-Disposition"],  # Needed for Redlined PDF file download
)

# Core routers (Modules 1 & 2)
app.include_router(session_router)
app.include_router(vision_router)

# Auto-mount Round 3 routers (Modules 3, 4, 5 & Devraj's KB router) as soon as they exist
for _mod_name in ("pdf_engine", "collab_router", "rules_router", "webhook_router", "kb_router"):
    try:
        _mod = __import__(_mod_name)
        if hasattr(_mod, "router"):
            app.include_router(_mod.router)
    except ImportError:
        pass


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Collision Point 4: Persistence Helpers
def save_scan_persistence(
    scan_resp: Dict[str, Any],
    redacted_text: str,
    input_type: str = "text",
    pdf_bytes: Optional[bytes] = None,
    file_sha256: Optional[str] = None,
    actor_name: str = "Creator",
    rag_results: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
) -> None:
    """Write the scan to memory immediately and persist it without blocking callers."""
    scan_id = scan_resp["scan_id"]
    payload = {
        **scan_resp,
        "redacted_text": redacted_text,
        "input_type": input_type,
    }

    # 1. Always keep a lightweight copy (without pdf_bytes) in bounded memory
    _mem_scans[scan_id] = payload
    _mem_scans.move_to_end(scan_id)
    while len(_mem_scans) > _MEM_SCANS_LIMIT:
        _mem_scans.popitem(last=False)
    try:
        import collab_router
        collab_router._memory_versions.setdefault(scan_id, [{
            "version_no": 1,
            "text_snapshot": redacted_text or "",
            "score": int(scan_resp.get("readiness_score", 100)),
            "actor_name": actor_name or "Creator",
            "created_at": "",
        }])
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        import pdf_engine
        pdf_engine._mem_scan_json[scan_id] = payload
        pdf_engine._mem_scan_json.move_to_end(scan_id)
        while len(pdf_engine._mem_scan_json) > 100:
            pdf_engine._mem_scan_json.popitem(last=False)
    except Exception:
        pass

    effective_rag = rag_results or {
        "verified_claims": scan_resp.get("verified_claims") or [],
        "issues": [i for i in (scan_resp.get("issues") or []) if i.get("evidence")],
    }

    def _db_persist_worker() -> None:
        conn = None
        try:
            conn = db._connect()
            if not conn:
                return
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO scans (scan_id, readiness_score, status, input_type, payload)
                       VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s) ON CONFLICT (scan_id) DO NOTHING;""",
                    (scan_id, int(scan_resp.get("readiness_score", 100)), str(scan_resp.get("status", "needs_review")), input_type, db.Json(payload)),
                )
                if pdf_bytes and file_sha256:
                    cur.execute(
                        "SELECT 1 FROM public.scan_documents WHERE file_sha256 = %s AND pdf_bytes IS NOT NULL LIMIT 1;",
                        (file_sha256,),
                    )
                    if cur.fetchone():
                        stored_pdf_bytes = None
                    else:
                        stored_pdf_bytes = pdf_bytes
                else:
                    stored_pdf_bytes = pdf_bytes
                cur.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO public.scan_documents
                       (scan_id, workspace_id, channel, redacted_text, file_sha256, pdf_bytes, result_json, reaudit_needed)
                       VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s::jsonb, FALSE)
                       ON CONFLICT (scan_id) DO UPDATE SET result_json = EXCLUDED.result_json,
                       redacted_text = EXCLUDED.redacted_text;""",
                    (scan_id, scan_resp.get("workspace_id", "default"), scan_resp.get("channel", "general"),
                     redacted_text, file_sha256, stored_pdf_bytes, json.dumps(payload)),
                )
                cur.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO public.scan_versions (scan_id, version_no, text_snapshot, score, actor_name)
                       VALUES (%s, 1, %s, %s, %s) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;""",
                    (scan_id, redacted_text or "", int(scan_resp.get("readiness_score", 100)), actor_name or "Creator"),
                )
                cur.execute("DELETE FROM public.scan_documents WHERE created_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours';")
                cur.execute("DELETE FROM public.scan_cache WHERE created_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours';")
            conn.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            if conn:
                try:
                    conn.rollback()
                except Exception:
                    pass
            print(f"[main] scan persistence DB write skipped: {exc}")
        finally:
            if conn:
                conn.close()
            if effective_rag["verified_claims"] or effective_rag["issues"]:
                pipeline.record_evidence(scan_id, effective_rag)

    threading.Thread(target=_db_persist_worker, daemon=True, name="scan-persist").start()

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Text Scan (Cached + Rate-Limited)
@app.post("/api/scan-text", response_model=ScanResponse)
def scan_text(
    request: ScanTextRequest,
    background: BackgroundTasks,
    _client_ip: str = Depends(rate_limit_scan),
    session: SessionUser = Depends(get_current_session),
):
    original_text = request.text
    if not original_text or not original_text.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Draft text cannot be empty.")

    channel = normalize_channel(request.channel)
    workspace_id = resolve_workspace_id(request.workspace_id, session)

    # Compute current versions for deterministic SHA-256 cache lookup
    rules, _facts, _src = db.get_workspace_vault(workspace_id)
    rules_version = get_rules_version()
    kb_version = pipeline.get_kb_version(workspace_id)

    cache_key = cache.make_cache_key(
        text=original_text,
        channel=channel,
        workspace_id=workspace_id,
        rules_version=rules_version,
        kb_version=kb_version,
    )
    cached_hit = cache.get_cached_scan(cache_key)
    if cached_hit:
        cached_hit["scan_id"] = new_scan_id()
        cached_hit["cached"] = True
        cached_hit["channel"] = channel
        cached_hit["workspace_id"] = workspace_id
        cached_response = ScanResponse(**cached_hit)
        background.add_task(
            save_scan_persistence,
            cached_response.model_dump(),
            cached_response.redacted_text or original_text,
            "text",
            None,
            None,
            session.name,
            None,
        )
        return cached_response

    analysis = pipeline.analyze_text(
        original_text,
        with_positions=True,
        channel=channel,
        workspace_id=workspace_id,
    )
    score, pillars, status = score_issues(analysis.issues, ai_ok=analysis.ai_ok)
    scan_id = new_scan_id()

    response_obj = ScanResponse(
        scan_id=scan_id,
        readiness_score=score,
        status=status,
        ai_status="ok" if analysis.ai_ok else "degraded",
        pillar_scores=pillars,
        ai_disclosure=pipeline.disclosure(analysis.model_label, analysis.ai_ok),
        pii_redacted_count=analysis.pii_count,
        issues=analysis.issues,
        input_type="text",
        redacted_text=analysis.redacted_text,
        cached=False,
        channel=channel,
        workspace_id=workspace_id,
        kb_version=analysis.kb_version,
        rules_version=analysis.rules_version,
        page_count=1,
        verified_claims=analysis.verified_claims,
        reaudit=ReauditInfo(needed=False, note=None),
    )

    resp_dict = response_obj.model_dump()

    # Only cache when AI tier succeeded (ai_status == "ok")
    if response_obj.ai_status == "ok":
        background.add_task(cache.set_cached_scan, cache_key, resp_dict)

    background.add_task(
        save_scan_persistence,
        resp_dict,
        analysis.redacted_text,
        "text",
        None,
        None,
        session.name,
        analysis.rag_results,
    )
    return response_obj

@app.get("/api/analytics/summary")
def analytics_summary():
    """Summary metrics for the governance dashboard."""
    total_scans, avg_score, approved_count, reaudit_count = 0, 0.0, 0, 0
    try:
        conn = db._connect()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"[main] analytics DB connection fallback: {exc}")
        conn = None
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*), COALESCE(AVG(readiness_score), 0), "
                    "COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'approved') FROM public.scans;"
                )
                row = cur.fetchone()
                if row:
                    total_scans, avg_score, approved_count = int(row[0]), round(float(row[1]), 1), int(row[2])
                cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.scan_documents WHERE reaudit_needed = TRUE;")
                r_row = cur.fetchone()
                if r_row:
                    reaudit_count = int(r_row[0])
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[main] analytics query fallback: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()
    else:
        vals = list(_mem_scans.values())
        total_scans = len(vals)
        avg_score = round(sum(int(v.get("readiness_score", 0)) for v in vals) / max(total_scans, 1), 1)
        approved_count = sum(1 for v in vals if v.get("status") == "approved")
        reaudit_count = sum(1 for v in vals if (v.get("reaudit") or {}).get("needed"))

    return {
        "total_scans": total_scans,
        "average_readiness_score": avg_score,
        "approved_scans": approved_count,
        "reaudit_queue_count": reaudit_count,
        "rules_version": get_rules_version(),
        "ambiguity_radar": get_ambiguity_radar(suggest=False)["ambiguity_radar"],
    }


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Guideline Ambiguity Radar
@app.post("/api/flag-action")
def log_flag_action(req: FlagActionRequest):
    if req.rule_id not in db.rules_by_id():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Unknown rule_id {req.rule_id}.")
    stored = db.log_flag_event(req.scan_id, req.rule_id, req.action, req.reason or "")
    return {"status": "logged", "rule_id": req.rule_id, "action": req.action, "stored_in": stored}


MIN_EVENTS = 3          # don't judge a rule from 1 or 2 clicks
OVERRIDE_LIMIT = 30.0   # Round 1 promise: more than 30% overrides = unclear rule


def _suggest_rewording(rule: dict, reasons: list) -> str:
    try:
        data, _ = ai_client.complete_json(
            'You are a brand-guideline editor. Reply with ONE JSON object: {"clearer_rule": "..."}.',
            f"This brand rule is often overridden by human reviewers.\nRule: {rule['rule_text']}\n"
            f"Override reasons: {reasons or ['(none given)']}\n"
            "Rewrite the rule in 1-2 clear sentences that state the exceptions explicitly.",
            max_tokens=800,
        )
        return str(data.get("clearer_rule", "")).strip() if isinstance(data, dict) else ""
    except Exception:
        return ""


@app.get("/api/analytics/ambiguity")
def get_ambiguity_radar(suggest: bool = False):
    """Rules with a high human-override rate. Add ?suggest=true to let the AI draft a clearer wording."""
    stats = {}
    for ev in db.get_flag_events():
        s = stats.setdefault(ev["rule_id"], {"total": 0, "overridden": 0, "reasons": []})
        s["total"] += 1
        if ev["action"] == "Overridden":
            s["overridden"] += 1
            if ev.get("reason"):
                s["reasons"].append(ev["reason"])

    rules = db.rules_by_id()
    results = []
    for rid, c in sorted(stats.items()):
        rule = rules.get(rid, {})
        rate = round(c["overridden"] / c["total"] * 100, 1)
        flagged = c["total"] >= MIN_EVENTS and rate > OVERRIDE_LIMIT
        item = {
            "rule_id": rid,
            "rule_cited": rule.get("rule_cited", f"Rule {rid}"),
            "source_ref": rule.get("source_ref", "Brand Guide"),
            "total_flags": c["total"],
            "override_count": c["overridden"],
            "override_rate_percent": rate,
            "needs_clarification": flagged,
            "enough_data": c["total"] >= MIN_EVENTS,
            "sample_override_reasons": c["reasons"][:3],
        }
        if suggest and flagged and rule:
            item["suggested_rewording"] = _suggest_rewording(rule, c["reasons"][:5]) or None
        results.append(item)
    return {"ambiguity_radar": results}


@app.get("/api/extension/download")
def download_chrome_extension():
    """Package the ClaimLoom Chrome Extension bundle as a downloadable zip archive."""
    ext_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "chrome-extension"
    if not ext_dir.is_dir():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chrome extension bundle not found.")

    zip_buffer = io.BytesIO()
    target_files = ["manifest.json", "popup.html", "popup.css", "popup.js"]
    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_buffer, mode="w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        for fname in target_files:
            fpath = ext_dir / fname
            if fpath.is_file():
                zf.write(fpath, arcname=fname)

    return Response(
        content=zip_buffer.getvalue(),
        media_type="application/zip",
        headers={"Content-Disposition": 'attachment; filename="ClaimLoom-Chrome-Extension.zip"'},
    )
