"""Proxy-aware Token Bucket Rate Limiter for Render Free Web Service.
Reads the first IP in X-Forwarded-For (never relying solely on request.client.host,
which is Render's internal load balancer) and bounds memory usage for Render's 512 MB limit."""
import math
import os
import threading
import time
from typing import Dict, Tuple

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request

# Generous defaults for live hackathon judging while blocking automated floods
BUCKET_CAPACITY = float(os.getenv("RATE_LIMIT_CAPACITY", "30"))       # burst of 30 scans
REFILL_RATE = float(os.getenv("RATE_LIMIT_REFILL_PER_SEC", "0.1"))    # refills 30 tokens every 5 minutes
MAX_TRACKED_IPS = 2048                                                # strict cap for Render 512 MB RAM

_lock = threading.Lock()
# ip -> (tokens_remaining, last_updated_monotonic)
_buckets: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]] = {}


def get_client_ip(request: Request) -> str:
    """Extracts the client IP from the last proxy-appended X-Forwarded-For entry."""
    xff = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for") or request.headers.get("X-Forwarded-For")
    if xff:
        last_ip = xff.split(",")[-1].strip()
        if last_ip:
            return last_ip
    real_ip = request.headers.get("x-real-ip")
    if real_ip and real_ip.strip():
        return real_ip.strip()
    if request.client and request.client.host:
        return request.client.host
    return "127.0.0.1"


def _prune_stale_buckets(now: float) -> None:
    """Evicts oldest or full buckets when table exceeds MAX_TRACKED_IPS."""
    if len(_buckets) <= MAX_TRACKED_IPS:
        return
    stale_cutoff = now - 300.0  # 5 minutes idle
    stale_keys = [ip for ip, (_, ts) in _buckets.items() if ts < stale_cutoff]
    for k in stale_keys:
        _buckets.pop(k, None)
    if len(_buckets) > MAX_TRACKED_IPS:
        # Drop oldest half if still over capacity
        sorted_ips = sorted(_buckets.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1][1])
        for ip, _ in sorted_ips[: len(_buckets) // 2]:
            _buckets.pop(ip, None)


def consume_token(ip: str, cost: float = 1.0) -> Tuple[bool, float, float]:
    """Consumes `cost` tokens from `ip`'s bucket.
    Returns (allowed, remaining_tokens, retry_after_seconds)."""
    now = time.monotonic()
    with _lock:
        _prune_stale_buckets(now)
        tokens, last_ts = _buckets.get(ip, (BUCKET_CAPACITY, now))
        elapsed = max(0.0, now - last_ts)
        tokens = min(BUCKET_CAPACITY, tokens + elapsed * REFILL_RATE)

        if tokens >= cost:
            tokens -= cost
            _buckets[ip] = (tokens, now)
            return True, round(tokens, 2), 0.0

        deficit = cost - tokens
        retry_after = deficit / REFILL_RATE if REFILL_RATE > 0 else 5.0
        _buckets[ip] = (tokens, now)
        return False, round(tokens, 2), round(retry_after, 2)


def rate_limit_scan(request: Request) -> str:
    """FastAPI dependency for scan endpoints. Raises HTTP 429 with Retry-After if bucket is empty."""
    ip = get_client_ip(request)
    global_capacity = 120.0
    global_refill = global_capacity / 300.0
    now = time.monotonic()
    with _lock:
        tokens, last_ts = _buckets.get("_GLOBAL_", (global_capacity, now))
        tokens = min(global_capacity, tokens + max(0.0, now - last_ts) * global_refill)
        if tokens < 1.0:
            retry_after = (1.0 - tokens) / global_refill
            _buckets["_GLOBAL_"] = (tokens, now)
            wait_sec = max(1, int(math.ceil(retry_after)))
            raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail="Global scan rate limit exceeded.", headers={"Retry-After": str(wait_sec)})
        _buckets["_GLOBAL_"] = (tokens - 1.0, now)
    allowed, _remaining, retry_after = consume_token(ip, cost=1.0)
    if not allowed:
        wait_sec = max(1, int(math.ceil(retry_after)))
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=429,
            detail=f"Rate limit exceeded for IP {ip}. Please retry in {wait_sec}s.",
            headers={"Retry-After": str(wait_sec)},
        )
    return ip


def reset_rate_limits() -> None:
    """Clears all rate-limit buckets (used by unit tests)."""
    with _lock:
        _buckets.clear()

FILE: rule_version.py

"""Lightweight version counter backed by the non-RAG rule history table."""
from __future__ import annotations

import time

import db

_cached_at = 0.0
_cached_version = 1
_TTL_SECONDS = 60.0


def get_rules_version(force: bool = False) -> int:
    global _cached_at, _cached_version
    now = time.monotonic()
    if not force and now - _cached_at < _TTL_SECONDS:
        return _cached_version
    version = 1
    try:
        conn = db._connect()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"[rule_version] database connection fallback: {exc}")
        conn = None
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(version_no), 1) FROM public.rule_versions")
                row = cur.fetchone()
                version = max(1, int(row[0] or 1)) if row else 1
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[rule_version] history version lookup fallback: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()
    if version <= 1:
        rules, _facts, _source = db.get_vault()
        version = max((int(rule.get("version_no") or 1) for rule in rules), default=1)
    _cached_version = version
    _cached_at = now
    return version


def invalidate_rules_version() -> None:
    global _cached_at
    _cached_at = 0.0


def set_rules_version(version: int) -> None:
    """Update the process cache after a local rules mutation."""
    global _cached_at, _cached_version
    _cached_version = max(1, int(version))
    _cached_at = time.monotonic()


========================================================================================
FILE: rules_router.py
========================================================================================
"""Self-healing brand-rule suggestions, safe backtests, edits, and rollback."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import AliasChoices, BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

import ai_client
import cache
import db
from engine import redact_pii_same_length
from session import SessionUser, get_current_session, require_role
from rule_version import get_rules_version as _global_rules_version, set_rules_version

router = APIRouter()

MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH = 512
MAX_BACKTEST_SCANS = 100
MAX_BACKTEST_TEXT_CHARS = 4000
_memory_history: Dict[int, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
_history_loaded: set[int] = set()
_past_scan_texts_cache: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
_past_scan_texts_cached_at = 0.0
_PAST_SCAN_TEXTS_TTL = 60.0


def _safe_connect():
    try:
        return db._connect()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"[rules_router] database connection fallback: {exc}")
        return None


class BacktestRequest(BaseModel):
    regex_pattern: Optional[str] = Field(
        default=None,
        max_length=MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH,
        validation_alias=AliasChoices("proposed_regex", "regex_pattern"),
    )


class RulePatch(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    violation_type: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=50)
    severity: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=20)
    rule_cited: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=500)
    source_ref: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=500)
    rule_text: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=4000, validation_alias=AliasChoices("rule_text", "proposed_rule_text"))
    regex_pattern: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH, validation_alias=AliasChoices("regex_pattern", "proposed_regex"))
    default_fix: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=1000)
    claim_status: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=100)
    allowed_numbers: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=500)
    applies_to: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, pattern=r"^(text|image|both)$")


class RollbackRequest(BaseModel):
    version_no: Optional[int] = Field(default=None, ge=1)


class RuleCreate(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    violation_type: str = Field(..., pattern=r"^(Factual|Tone|Terminology|Accessibility|Visual)$")
    severity: str = Field(default="Medium", pattern=r"^(High|Medium|Low)$")
    rule_cited: str = Field(..., min_length=3, max_length=500)
    source_ref: str = Field(default="Custom Workspace Guideline", max_length=500)
    rule_text: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=4000)
    regex_pattern: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH)
    default_fix: Optional[str] = Field(default="compliant phrasing", max_length=1000)
    applies_to: str = Field(default="text", pattern=r"^(text|image|both)$")
    workspace_id: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=64)


@router.post("/api/rules")
def create_custom_rule(
    req: RuleCreate,
    session: SessionUser = Depends(require_role("Admin")),
):
    _validate_regex(req.regex_pattern)
    if req.workspace_id and req.workspace_id != session.workspace_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Rule workspace must match the active session workspace.")
    rules, _, _ = db.get_vault()
    next_id = max((int(r.get("rule_id") or 0) for r in rules), default=20) + 1
    new_rule = {
        "rule_id": next_id,
        "workspace_id": session.workspace_id,
        **req.model_dump(),
        "claim_status": None,
        "allowed_numbers": None,
    }
    next_version = _global_rules_version() + 1
    new_rule["version_no"] = next_version
    conn = _safe_connect()
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO public.brand_rules
                       (rule_id, workspace_id, version_no, violation_type, severity, rule_cited, source_ref, rule_text, regex_pattern, default_fix, applies_to)
                       VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)""",
                    (
                        next_id, session.workspace_id, next_version, req.violation_type, req.severity, req.rule_cited,
                        req.source_ref, req.rule_text, req.regex_pattern,
                        req.default_fix, req.applies_to,
                    ),
                )
            conn.commit()
            cached_rules = db._cache.setdefault("rules", [])
            cached_rules[:] = [r for r in cached_rules if int(r.get("rule_id", -1)) != next_id]
            cached_rules.append(new_rule)
        except Exception as exc:
            conn.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=f"Could not create rule: {exc}") from exc
        finally:
            conn.close()
    else:
        db._cache.setdefault("rules", []).append(new_rule)
    set_rules_version(next_version)
    cache.invalidate_cache()
    return {"status": "created", "rule": new_rule, "rules_version": _global_rules_version()}


def _get_rule(rule_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    rule = db.rules_by_id().get(rule_id)
    if not rule:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Unknown rule_id {rule_id}.")
    return dict(rule)


def _parse_scan_payload(payload: Any) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    if not payload:
        return None
    try:
        value = payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else json.loads(payload)
        if not isinstance(value, dict):
            return None
        text = value.get("redacted_text") or value.get("text_snapshot") or ""
        return {"scan_id": value.get("scan_id"), "text": str(text)} if text else None
    except Exception:
        return None


def _past_scan_texts() -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    global _past_scan_texts_cached_at, _past_scan_texts_cache
    now = time.monotonic()
    try:
        import main
        memory_samples = [
            {"scan_id": str(scan_id), "text": str(data.get("redacted_text") or "")}
            for scan_id, data in reversed(main._mem_scans.items())
            if data.get("redacted_text")
        ][:MAX_BACKTEST_SCANS]
    except Exception:
        memory_samples = []
    if memory_samples:
        result = [{**s, "text": s["text"][:MAX_BACKTEST_TEXT_CHARS]} for s in memory_samples]
        _past_scan_texts_cache = result
        _past_scan_texts_cached_at = now
        return [dict(item) for item in result]
    if now - _past_scan_texts_cached_at < _PAST_SCAN_TEXTS_TTL:
        return [dict(item) for item in _past_scan_texts_cache]
    samples: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
    conn = _safe_connect()
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT scan_id, payload FROM public.scans ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT %s", (MAX_BACKTEST_SCANS,))
                for scan_id, payload in cur.fetchall():
                    item = _parse_scan_payload(payload)
                    if item:
                        item["scan_id"] = str(scan_id)
                        samples.append(item)
                if len(samples) < MAX_BACKTEST_SCANS:
                    cur.execute(
                        "SELECT scan_id, redacted_text FROM public.scan_documents ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT %s",
                        (MAX_BACKTEST_SCANS - len(samples),),
                    )
                    seen = {s["scan_id"] for s in samples}
                    for scan_id, text in cur.fetchall():
                        if text and str(scan_id) not in seen:
                            samples.append({"scan_id": str(scan_id), "text": str(text)})
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[rules_router] past scan load fallback: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()
    if not samples:
        try:
            import main
            samples = [
                {"scan_id": str(scan_id), "text": str(data.get("redacted_text") or "")}
                for scan_id, data in reversed(main._mem_scans.items())
                if data.get("redacted_text")
            ][:MAX_BACKTEST_SCANS]
        except Exception:
            pass
    result = [{**s, "text": s["text"][:MAX_BACKTEST_TEXT_CHARS]} for s in samples[:MAX_BACKTEST_SCANS]]
    _past_scan_texts_cache = result
    _past_scan_texts_cached_at = now
    return [dict(item) for item in result]


def _contains_nested_repeat_or_branch(nodes: Any, inside_repeat: bool = False) -> bool:
    """Conservative regex safety check: no quantified group containing another
    quantifier or alternation, the common source of catastrophic backtracking."""
    import re._constants as constants

    repeat_ops = {constants.MAX_REPEAT, constants.MIN_REPEAT}
    if hasattr(constants, "POSSESSIVE_REPEAT"):
        repeat_ops.add(constants.POSSESSIVE_REPEAT)
    for op, arg in nodes:
        if op in repeat_ops:
            _min_repeat, max_repeat, child = arg
            unbounded = max_repeat == constants.MAXREPEAT
            if inside_repeat and unbounded:
                return True
            if _contains_nested_repeat_or_branch(child, inside_repeat=inside_repeat or unbounded):
                return True
        elif op is constants.SUBPATTERN:
            if _contains_nested_repeat_or_branch(arg[-1], inside_repeat=inside_repeat):
                return True
        elif op is constants.BRANCH:
            if inside_repeat or any(_contains_nested_repeat_or_branch(branch, inside_repeat=inside_repeat) for branch in arg[1]):
                return True
    return False


def _validate_regex(pattern: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:
    if pattern is None or pattern == "":
        return None
    if len(pattern.strip()) < 3:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Regex pattern is too short/broad (min 3 chars).")
    if len(pattern) > MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=f"Regex exceeds {MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH} characters.")
    if re.search(r"\\[1-9]|\(\?P=", pattern):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Regex backreferences are not allowed in editable brand rules.")
    try:
        compiled = re.compile(pattern)
        if compiled.search("ClaimLoom Creators build compliant enterprise assets."):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Regex is overly broad and flags clean baseline copy.")
        import re._parser as parser
        parsed = parser.parse(pattern, 0)
        if _contains_nested_repeat_or_branch(parsed):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Regex has a nested or ambiguous repetition that could cause excessive backtracking.")
        return pattern
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except re.error as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=f"Invalid regex: {exc}") from exc


def _version_and_history(rule_id: int) -> Tuple[int, List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    if _history_loaded is True or rule_id in _history_loaded or rule_id in _memory_history:
        history = list(_memory_history.get(rule_id, []))
        return max((h["version_no"] for h in history), default=int(_get_rule(rule_id).get("version_no") or 1)), history
    conn = _safe_connect()
    if conn:
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT version_no, old_rule_text, old_pattern, changed_by, created_at FROM public.rule_versions WHERE rule_id = %s ORDER BY version_no ASC, id ASC", (rule_id,))
                history = [
                    {"version_no": int(r[0]), "rule_text": r[1], "regex_pattern": r[2], "changed_by": r[3], "created_at": str(r[4])}
                    for r in cur.fetchall()
                ]
                _memory_history[rule_id] = history
                _history_loaded.add(rule_id)
                return max((h["version_no"] for h in history), default=1), history
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[rules_router] rule history unavailable: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()
    history = list(_memory_history.get(rule_id, []))
    _history_loaded.add(rule_id)
    return max((h["version_no"] for h in history), default=int(_get_rule(rule_id).get("version_no") or 1)), history


def _save_rule_change(rule_id: int, old_rule: Dict[str, Any], new_values: Dict[str, Any], changed_by: str) -> int:
    current_version, _ = _version_and_history(rule_id)
    next_version = _global_rules_version() + 1
    history = _memory_history.setdefault(rule_id, [])
    history.append({"version_no": current_version, "rule_text": old_rule.get("rule_text"), "regex_pattern": old_rule.get("regex_pattern"), "changed_by": changed_by, "created_at": None})
    history.append({"version_no": next_version, "rule_text": new_values.get("rule_text", old_rule.get("rule_text")), "regex_pattern": new_values.get("regex_pattern", old_rule.get("regex_pattern")), "changed_by": changed_by, "created_at": None})
    _history_loaded.add(rule_id)
    rules = db._cache.setdefault("rules", [])
    for rule in rules:
        if int(rule.get("rule_id", -1)) == rule_id:
            rule.update(new_values)
            rule["version_no"] = next_version
            break
    else:
        rules.append({**old_rule, **new_values, "rule_id": rule_id, "version_no": next_version})
    # Keep local changes in the rule cache even while Neon persistence is pending.
    db._cache["at"] = time.time()
    db._cache["ttl"] = max(int(db._cache.get("ttl") or 0), 24 * 60 * 60)

    def _persist_rule_change() -> None:
        conn = _safe_connect()
        if not conn:
            return
        try:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO public.rule_versions (rule_id, version_no, old_rule_text, old_pattern, changed_by) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)",
                    (rule_id, current_version, old_rule.get("rule_text"), old_rule.get("regex_pattern"), changed_by),
                )
                assignments = ", ".join(f"{column} = %s" for column in new_values)
                values = [new_values[column] for column in new_values]
                cur.execute(f"UPDATE public.brand_rules SET {assignments} WHERE rule_id = %s", (*values, rule_id))
                if cur.rowcount == 0:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Unknown rule_id {rule_id}")
                cur.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO public.rule_versions (rule_id, version_no, old_rule_text, old_pattern, changed_by) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)",
                    (rule_id, next_version, new_values.get("rule_text", old_rule.get("rule_text")), new_values.get("regex_pattern", old_rule.get("regex_pattern")), changed_by),
                )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            conn.rollback()
            print(f"[rules_router] rule persistence skipped: {exc}")
        finally:
            conn.close()

    import threading
    threading.Thread(target=_persist_rule_change, daemon=True, name="rule-change-persist").start()
    set_rules_version(next_version)
    cache.invalidate_cache()
    return next_version


@router.post("/api/rules/{rule_id}/rewrite")
def rewrite_rule(rule_id: int, session: SessionUser = Depends(require_role("Reviewer", "Admin"))):
    rule = _get_rule(rule_id)
    event_reasons = [
        str(event.get("reason") or "").strip()
        for event in db.get_flag_events()
        if int(event.get("rule_id") or -1) == rule_id
        and event.get("action") in {"Overridden", "Dismissed"}
        and str(event.get("reason") or "").strip()
    ][:12]
    event_reasons = [redact_pii_same_length(reason)[0] for reason in event_reasons]
    system = (
        "You are a precise brand-governance rule editor. Return one JSON object with keys "
        "proposed_rule_text, proposed_regex, default_fix, and rationale. Preserve the rule's policy intent. "
        "Use null for proposed_regex when a deterministic regex is unsuitable. Do not invent policy facts."
    )
    prompt = (
        f"Rewrite this rule for clarity and a deterministic text detector where appropriate.\n"
        f"Rule ID: {rule_id}\nType: {rule.get('violation_type')}\n"
        f"Current rule: {rule.get('rule_text')}\nCurrent regex: {rule.get('regex_pattern')}\n"
        f"Current fix: {rule.get('default_fix')}\n"
        f"Reviewer override/dismissal reasons: {event_reasons or ['No recorded reasons']}"
    )
    try:
        result, model = ai_client.complete_json(system, prompt, max_tokens=900)
    except ai_client.AIUnavailable as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=f"Rule rewrite unavailable: {exc}") from exc
    proposed_text = result.get("proposed_rule_text", result.get("rule_text")) if isinstance(result, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(result, dict) or not isinstance(proposed_text, str) or not proposed_text.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="AI returned an invalid rule rewrite.")
    candidate_pattern = result.get("proposed_regex", result.get("regex_pattern"))
    if candidate_pattern is not None and not isinstance(candidate_pattern, str):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="AI returned a non-string regex pattern.")
    _validate_regex(candidate_pattern)
    return {
        "rule_id": rule_id,
        "current": {"rule_text": rule.get("rule_text"), "regex_pattern": rule.get("regex_pattern"), "default_fix": rule.get("default_fix")},
        "suggestion": {
            "rule_text": proposed_text.strip(),
            "regex_pattern": candidate_pattern,
            "default_fix": result.get("default_fix"),
            "rationale": str(result.get("rationale") or "").strip(),
        },
        "model": model,
        "applied": False,
        "proposed_rule_text": proposed_text.strip(),
        "proposed_regex": candidate_pattern,
    }


@router.post("/api/rules/{rule_id}/backtest")
def backtest_rule(rule_id: int, req: BacktestRequest, session: SessionUser = Depends(require_role("Reviewer", "Admin"))):
    rule = _get_rule(rule_id)
    pattern = req.regex_pattern if req.regex_pattern is not None else rule.get("regex_pattern")
    _validate_regex(pattern)
    try:
        compiled = re.compile(pattern) if pattern else None
        old_pattern = rule.get("regex_pattern")
        old_compiled = re.compile(old_pattern) if old_pattern else None
    except re.error as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=f"Invalid regex: {exc}") from exc

    samples = _past_scan_texts()
    default_fix = str(rule.get("default_fix") or "").strip()
    if compiled and default_fix and compiled.search(default_fix):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Proposed regex matches this rule's own suggested_fix.")
    candidate_ids: List[str] = []
    existing_ids: List[str] = []
    examples: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
    candidate_match_total = 0
    existing_match_total = 0
    for sample in samples:
        text = sample["text"]
        candidate = list(compiled.finditer(text))[:20] if compiled else []
        existing = list(old_compiled.finditer(text))[:20] if old_compiled else []
        candidate_match_total += len(candidate)
        existing_match_total += len(existing)
        if candidate:
            candidate_ids.append(sample["scan_id"])
            if len(examples) < 10:
                examples.append({"scan_id": sample["scan_id"], "match": candidate[0].group(0)[:160]})
        if existing:
            existing_ids.append(sample["scan_id"])
    candidate_set, old_set = set(candidate_ids), set(existing_ids)
    eliminated = sorted(old_set - candidate_set)
    return {
        "rule_id": rule_id,
        "regex_pattern": pattern,
        "proposed_regex": pattern,
        "valid_regex": True,
        "scans_sampled": len(samples),
        "scans_with_matches": len(candidate_set),
        "total_matches_capped_per_scan": candidate_match_total,
        "scans_evaluated": len(samples),
        "previous_flags": existing_match_total,
        "new_flags": candidate_match_total,
        "false_positives_eliminated": len(eliminated),
        "scans_newly_flagged": sorted(candidate_set - old_set),
        "scans_no_longer_flagged": sorted(old_set - candidate_set),
        "example_matches": examples,
        "safety": "passed" if pattern is None or pattern == "" or compiled is not None else "failed",
        "note": "Backtest compares regex matches against up to 100 recent stored scan texts. 'false_positives_eliminated' is a match-count proxy, not a human-labeled precision metric.",
    }


@router.patch("/api/rules/{rule_id}")
def update_rule(
    rule_id: int,
    req: RulePatch,
    session: SessionUser = Depends(require_role("Admin")),
):
    old_rule = _get_rule(rule_id)
    updates = req.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
    if not updates:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Provide at least one rule field to update.")
    if "severity" in updates and updates["severity"] not in {"High", "Medium", "Low"}:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="severity must be High, Medium, or Low.")
    if "violation_type" in updates and updates["violation_type"] not in {"Factual", "Tone", "Terminology", "Accessibility", "Visual"}:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Unsupported violation_type.")
    if "regex_pattern" in updates:
        updates["regex_pattern"] = _validate_regex(updates["regex_pattern"])
    version_no = _save_rule_change(rule_id, old_rule, updates, session.name)
    refreshed = _get_rule(rule_id)
    refreshed["version_no"] = version_no
    return {"status": "updated", "version_no": version_no, "rule": refreshed}


@router.post("/api/rules/{rule_id}/rollback")
def rollback_rule(
    rule_id: int,
    req: RollbackRequest = RollbackRequest(),
    session: SessionUser = Depends(require_role("Admin")),
):
    current = _get_rule(rule_id)
    current_version, history = _version_and_history(rule_id)
    earlier = [h for h in history if h["version_no"] < current_version]
    target_version = req.version_no if req.version_no is not None else max((h["version_no"] for h in earlier), default=0)
    if target_version < 1 or target_version >= current_version:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Rollback target must be an earlier rule version.")
    candidates = [h for h in history if h["version_no"] == target_version]
    if not candidates:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Rule version {target_version} was not found.")
    target = candidates[-1]
    # The history table tracks the two fields it was designed for. Keep every other
    # current field as-is, while recording this rollback as a new rule version.
    updates = {"rule_text": target.get("rule_text"), "regex_pattern": target.get("regex_pattern")}
    _validate_regex(updates["regex_pattern"])
    new_version = _save_rule_change(rule_id, current, updates, session.name)
    refreshed = _get_rule(rule_id)
    refreshed["version_no"] = new_version
    return {"status": "rolled_back", "from_version": current_version, "restored_version": target_version, "version_no": new_version, "rule": refreshed}

FILE: schemas.py

"""Shared API v3 contract (what Rohit's frontend receives) + deterministic scoring.
Maintains 100% backward compatibility with Round 2 (IssueItem alias, PillarScores, Region)
while adding all Round 3 Collision Point 1 & 4 fields with safe defaults."""
import uuid
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional, Union

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, model_validator


class Region(BaseModel):
    x: int
    y: int
    w: int
    h: int


class EvidenceCitation(BaseModel):
    verdict: str  # "Verified" | "Contradicted" | "Unsupported"
    doc_title: str
    page: int = 1
    paragraph: int = 1
    excerpt: str
    similarity: float
    chunk_id: Optional[int] = None


class Issue(BaseModel):
    # Existing Round 2 fields (kept intact for backward compatibility)
    id: str = ""
    severity: str  # "High" | "Medium" | "Low"
    violation_type: str  # "Factual" | "Tone" | "Terminology" | "Accessibility" | "Visual"
    flagged_text: str
    start: Optional[int] = 0   # -1 means "no text position, show card without highlight"
    end: Optional[int] = 0     # UTF-16 units so JavaScript highlighting matches exactly
    region: Optional[Any] = None
    rule_id: Optional[int] = None
    rule_cited: str
    source_ref: str
    explanation: str
    suggested_fix: str
    confidence: float
    detected_by: str  # "rule_engine" | "ai" | "rag_verifier" | "pdf_layout"
    claim_status: Optional[str] = None

    # New Round 3 fields for Collision Point 1
    page: Optional[int] = None  # 1-based page number (null for plain text)
    bbox: Optional[List[float]] = None  # [x0, y0, x1, y1] normalized 0.0 to 1.0
    evidence: Optional[EvidenceCitation] = None
    channel: Optional[str] = "general"


# Backward-compatible alias so vision_router.py, engine.py, and existing tests never break
IssueItem = Issue


class PillarScores(BaseModel):
    tone: int = 100
    terminology: int = 100
    claims: int = 100
    accessibility: int = 100
    visual: int = 100


class ReauditInfo(BaseModel):
    needed: bool = False
    note: Optional[str] = None


class ScanResponse(BaseModel):
    # Existing Round 2 fields
    scan_id: str
    readiness_score: int
    status: str  # "approved" | "needs_review" | "needs_reaudit"
    ai_status: str = "ok"  # "ok" | "degraded"
    pillar_scores: Union[PillarScores, Dict[str, int]]
    ai_disclosure: str
    pii_redacted_count: int = 0
    issues: List[Issue]

    # New Round 3 fields for Collision Point 1 & 4
    input_type: str = "text"  # "text" | "pdf" | "image"
    redacted_text: Optional[str] = None  # Returned on GET /api/scans/{id} for reload
    cached: bool = False
    channel: str = "general"
    workspace_id: str = "default"
    kb_version: int = 1
    rules_version: int = 1
    page_count: int = 1
    verified_claims: List[EvidenceCitation] = Field(default_factory=list)
    reaudit: ReauditInfo = Field(default_factory=ReauditInfo)


class ScanTextRequest(BaseModel):
    text: str = Field(..., max_length=8000, description="Draft content to scan")
    channel: str = Field(default="general", description="Target publication channel")
    workspace_id: str = Field(default="default", description="Workspace identifier")


class FlagActionRequest(BaseModel):
    scan_id: str
    rule_id: int
    action: Literal["Accepted", "Dismissed", "Overridden"]
    reason: Optional[str] = ""

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def reason_needed_for_override(self):
        # Round 1 promise: "Human Override with Justification"
        if self.action == "Overridden" and not (self.reason or "").strip():
            raise ValueError("A reason is required when overriding a flag.")
        return self


ALLOWED_CHANNELS = {"general", "linkedin", "instagram", "billboard", "email"}

WEIGHTS = {"High": 20, "Medium": 12, "Low": 6}
PILLAR_OF = {
    "Tone": "tone",
    "Terminology": "terminology",
    "Factual": "claims",
    "Accessibility": "accessibility",
    "Visual": "visual",
}


def normalize_channel(channel: Optional[str]) -> str:
    cleaned = (channel or "general").strip().lower()
    return cleaned if cleaned in ALLOWED_CHANNELS else "general"


def new_scan_id() -> str:
    return f"scan_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"


def score_issues(issues: List[Issue], ai_ok: bool = True):
    """Returns (overall_score, pillar_scores, status).
    Overall = 100 minus ALL deductions, so multiple problems lower the score."""
    deductions = {p: 0 for p in PILLAR_OF.values()}
    total = 0
    for iss in issues:
        w = WEIGHTS.get(iss.severity, 12)
        pillar_key = PILLAR_OF.get(iss.violation_type, "claims")
        deductions[pillar_key] += w
        total += w
    pillars = PillarScores(**{p: max(0, 100 - d) for p, d in deductions.items()})
    overall = max(0, 100 - total)
    has_blocker = any(i.severity in ("High", "Medium") for i in issues)
    status = "approved" if (overall >= 90 and not has_blocker and ai_ok) else "needs_review"
    return overall, pillars, status

# Define your rule columns explicitly so they match your dictionaries
RULE_COLUMNS = [
    "rule_id",
    "workspace_id",
    "version_no",
    "violation_type",
    "severity",
    "rule_cited",
    "source_ref",
    "rule_text",
    "regex_pattern",

    "claim_status",
    "allowed_numbers",
    "applies_to",
]

for _rule in RULES_DATA:
    _rule.setdefault("workspace_id", "default")
    _rule.setdefault("version_no", 1)
    _rule.setdefault("allowed_numbers", None)
    _rule.setdefault("applies_to", "text")


def seed_neon_database():
    import psycopg2

    db_url = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL")
    if not db_url or "ep-xyz" in db_url or "user:password" in db_url:
        print("No real DATABASE_URL set. Skipping seed (the app will use the local fallback lists).")
        return

    conn = psycopg2.connect(db_url, connect_timeout=10)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    migration_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "migrations", "001_round3.sql")
    with open(migration_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as migration_file:
        for statement in migration_file.read().split(";"):
            if statement.strip():
                cur.execute(statement)

    cols = ", ".join(RULE_COLUMNS)
    marks = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(RULE_COLUMNS))
    updates = ", ".join(f"{c} = EXCLUDED.{c}" for c in RULE_COLUMNS if c != "rule_id")  # update EVERY column
    for r in RULES_DATA:
        cur.execute(
            f"INSERT INTO brand_rules ({cols}) VALUES ({marks}) ON CONFLICT (rule_id) DO UPDATE SET {updates};",
            [r[c] for c in RULE_COLUMNS],
        )
    for f in FACTS_DATA:
        cur.execute(
            """INSERT INTO facts (fact_id, fact_text, source_ref) VALUES (%s, %s, %s)
               ON CONFLICT (fact_id) DO UPDATE SET fact_text = EXCLUDED.fact_text, source_ref = EXCLUDED.source_ref;""",
            (f["fact_id"], f["fact_text"], f["source_ref"]),
        )

    cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM flag_events;")
    if cur.fetchone()[0] == 0:
        for ev in DEMO_FLAG_EVENTS:
            cur.execute("INSERT INTO flag_events (scan_id, rule_id, action, reason) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s);", ev)

    cur.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.kb_meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1);")
    cur.execute("INSERT INTO public.kb_meta (key, value) VALUES ('kb_version', 1) ON CONFLICT (key) DO NOTHING;")
    conn.commit()
    cur.close()
    conn.close()
    print(f"Neon seeded: {len(RULES_DATA)} rules, {len(FACTS_DATA)} facts, tables scans + flag_events ready.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    seed_neon_database()

import json
from db import get_db_connection

def seed_demo_scans():
    conn = get_db_connection()
    try:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            # 1. Prepare two dummy pre-approved scans
            demo_scans = [
                ("demo_scan_1", "Our new Pro Plan is amazing at just $29/month!"),
                ("demo_scan_2", "Sign up today for $29/month to get the Pro Plan features.")
            ]
            
            for scan_id, text in demo_scans:
                mock_result = {
                    "scan_id": scan_id,
                    "status": "approved",
                    "readiness_score": 100,
                    "issues": []
                }
                
                # Insert into scan_documents
                cur.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO public.scan_documents 
                        (scan_id, workspace_id, channel, redacted_text, result_json, reaudit_needed)
                    VALUES (%s, 'default', 'general', %s, %s::jsonb, FALSE)
                    ON CONFLICT (scan_id) DO NOTHING;
                """, (scan_id, text, json.dumps(mock_result)))

                # 2. Link them to the Pricing chunk (chunk_id = 3) in claim_evidence
                # This ensures that when the judge changes the price to $39, these get flagged!
                cur.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO public.claim_evidence (scan_id, chunk_id, verdict, similarity)
                    VALUES (%s, 3, 'Verified', 0.95);
                """, (scan_id,))
        
        conn.commit()
        print("Successfully seeded the Demo Scans for the Live Price Change feature!")
    except Exception as e:
        conn.rollback()
        print(f"Error seeding demo scans: {e}")
    finally:
        conn.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    seed_demo_scans()

"""Seed searchable Truth Vault chunks without deleting evidence history."""
import hashlib

from seed_db import FACTS_DATA

KB_CHUNKS = [
    {"chunk_id": i, "doc_title": fact["source_ref"].split(", p.")[0], "page_num": int(fact["source_ref"].rsplit("p.", 1)[-1]) if "p." in fact["source_ref"] else 1, "paragraph_num": 1, "chunk_text": fact["fact_text"]}
    for i, fact in enumerate(FACTS_DATA, 1)
]


def seed_kb(conn):
    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.kb_chunks (
                chunk_id INT PRIMARY KEY,
                doc_title TEXT,
                page_num INT DEFAULT 1,
                paragraph_num INT DEFAULT 1,
                chunk_text TEXT,
                content_hash TEXT,
                embedding JSONB,
                workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default',
                updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );
        """)
        cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS doc_title TEXT;")
        cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS page_num INT DEFAULT 1;")
        cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS paragraph_num INT DEFAULT 1;")
        cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS chunk_text TEXT;")
        cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS content_hash TEXT;")
        cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS embedding JSONB;")
        cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default';")
        cur.execute("ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;")
        cur.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.kb_meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1);")
        cur.execute("INSERT INTO public.kb_meta (key, value) VALUES ('kb_version', 1) ON CONFLICT (key) DO NOTHING;")
        for chunk in KB_CHUNKS:
            digest = hashlib.sha256(chunk["chunk_text"].encode()).hexdigest()
            cur.execute("""INSERT INTO public.kb_chunks (chunk_id, doc_title, page_num, paragraph_num, chunk_text, content_hash, workspace_id) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,'default')
            ON CONFLICT (chunk_id) DO UPDATE SET doc_title=EXCLUDED.doc_title,page_num=EXCLUDED.page_num,paragraph_num=EXCLUDED.paragraph_num,chunk_text=EXCLUDED.chunk_text,content_hash=EXCLUDED.content_hash,workspace_id=EXCLUDED.workspace_id,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;""", (chunk["chunk_id"], chunk["doc_title"], chunk["page_num"], chunk["paragraph_num"], chunk["chunk_text"], digest))


========================================================================================
FILE: session.py

"""Stateless HMAC-SHA256 Role Session Tokens (Creator / Reviewer / Admin).
Provides POST /api/session to issue tokens and FastAPI dependencies to inspect
Authorization: Bearer <token> and X-Workspace-Id headers.
Falls back to Creator role if Authorization header is missing so basic curl tests never break."""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import os
import secrets
import time
from typing import Callable, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, HTTPException, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

router = APIRouter()

_database_url = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL", "")
SESSION_SECRET = os.getenv("SESSION_SECRET") or (hashlib.sha256(_database_url.encode()).hexdigest() if _database_url else secrets.token_hex(32))
TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS = int(os.getenv("SESSION_TTL_SECONDS", str(7 * 24 * 3600)))  # 7 days default

VALID_ROLES = {
    "creator": "Creator",
    "reviewer": "Reviewer",
    "admin": "Admin",
}


class SessionRequest(BaseModel):
    role: str = Field(default="Creator", description="Creator | Reviewer | Admin")
    name: str = Field(default="Demo User", max_length=80, description="Display name for audit trails")
    workspace_id: str = Field(default="default", max_length=64, description="Target workspace ID")


class SessionUser(BaseModel):
    name: str = "Creator"
    role: str = "Creator"  # "Creator" | "Reviewer" | "Admin"
    workspace_id: str = "default"
    iat: int = 0
    exp: int = 0
    authenticated: bool = False


class SessionResponse(BaseModel):
    token: str
    role: str
    name: str
    workspace_id: str
    expires_at: int


def _b64url_encode(data: bytes) -> str:
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(data).rstrip(b"=").decode("ascii")


def _b64url_decode(data: str) -> bytes:
    padding = "=" * (-len(data) % 4)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64decode((data + padding).encode("ascii"))


def normalize_role(role: Optional[str]) -> str:
    cleaned = (role or "Creator").strip().lower()
    if cleaned not in VALID_ROLES:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=f"Invalid role '{role}'. Must be one of: Creator, Reviewer, Admin.",
        )
    return VALID_ROLES[cleaned]


def create_session_token(name: str, role: str = "Creator", workspace_id: str = "default") -> Tuple_Token:
    norm_role = normalize_role(role)
    clean_name = (name or norm_role).strip()[:80] or norm_role
    clean_ws = (workspace_id or "default").strip()[:64] or "default"
    now = int(time.time())
    exp = now + TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS

    payload = {
        "sub": clean_name,
        "role": norm_role,
        "ws": clean_ws,
        "iat": now,
        "exp": exp,
    }
    payload_bytes = json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
    payload_b64 = _b64url_encode(payload_bytes)
    sig = hmac.new(SESSION_SECRET.encode("utf-8"), payload_b64.encode("ascii"), hashlib.sha256).digest()
    token = f"{payload_b64}.{_b64url_encode(sig)}"
    return token, norm_role, clean_name, clean_ws, exp


# Type alias helper for readability
Tuple_Token = tuple[str, str, str, str, int]


def verify_session_token(token: str) -> Optional[SessionUser]:
    """Verifies an HMAC-SHA256 token and returns SessionUser, or None if invalid/expired."""
    try:
        parts = (token or "").strip().split(".")
        if len(parts) != 2:
            return None
        payload_b64, sig_b64 = parts
        expected_sig = hmac.new(
            SESSION_SECRET.encode("utf-8"),
            payload_b64.encode("ascii"),
            hashlib.sha256,
        ).digest()
        actual_sig = _b64url_decode(sig_b64)
        if not hmac.compare_digest(expected_sig, actual_sig):
            return None

        payload = json.loads(_b64url_decode(payload_b64).decode("utf-8"))
        exp = int(payload.get("exp", 0))
        if exp and time.time() > exp:
            return None

        raw_role = str(payload.get("role", "Creator")).strip().lower()
        role = VALID_ROLES.get(raw_role, "Creator")
        return SessionUser(
            name=str(payload.get("sub") or role),
            role=role,
            workspace_id=str(payload.get("ws") or "default"),
            iat=int(payload.get("iat") or 0),
            exp=exp,
            authenticated=True,
        )
    except Exception:
        return None


def get_current_session(
    request: Request,
    authorization: Optional[str] = Header(default=None),
    x_workspace_id: Optional[str] = Header(default=None, alias="X-Workspace-Id"),
) -> SessionUser:
    """FastAPI dependency: extracts role & workspace from headers.
    Falls back to Creator role if Authorization header is missing so curl & Round 2 callers work."""
    ws_header = (x_workspace_id or "").strip()
    now = int(time.time())

    if authorization:
        scheme, _, param = authorization.strip().partition(" ")
        if scheme.lower() != "bearer" or not param:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Authorization must use a Bearer token.")
        raw_token = param
        user = verify_session_token(raw_token)
        if user:
            if ws_header and ws_header != user.workspace_id:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="X-Workspace-Id must match the workspace in the session token.")
            return user
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid or expired session token.")

    return SessionUser(
        name="Creator",
        role="Creator",
        workspace_id="default",
        iat=now,
        exp=now + TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS,
        authenticated=False,
    )


def require_role(*allowed_roles: str) -> Callable:
    """Dependency factory for endpoints that require Reviewer or Admin role.
    Example: Depends(require_role("Reviewer", "Admin"))"""
    normalized_allowed = {VALID_ROLES.get(r.lower(), r) for r in allowed_roles}

    def _checker(session: SessionUser = Depends(get_current_session)) -> SessionUser:
        if session.role not in normalized_allowed:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=403,
                detail=f"Role '{session.role}' is not authorized. Required: {', '.join(sorted(normalized_allowed))}.",
            )
        return session

    return _checker


def resolve_workspace_id(requested_workspace_id: Optional[str], session: SessionUser) -> str:
    """Resolve a requested workspace without allowing cross-workspace scans by default."""
    requested = (requested_workspace_id or "default").strip() or "default"
    own_workspace = (session.workspace_id or "default").strip() or "default"
    if requested == "default":
        return own_workspace
    if requested != own_workspace and session.role != "Admin":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="This session cannot access the requested workspace.")
    return requested


@router.post("/api/session", response_model=SessionResponse)
def issue_session(
    req: SessionRequest,
    issuer_secret: Optional[str] = Header(default=None, alias="X-Session-Issuer-Secret"),
):
    """Issues an HMAC-SHA256 role token for Creator, Reviewer, or Admin."""
    role = normalize_role(req.role)
    required_issuer_secret = os.getenv("SESSION_ISSUER_SECRET", "").strip()
    requested_workspace = (req.workspace_id or "default").strip() or "default"
    elevated_issuance = role != "Creator" or requested_workspace != "default"
    if elevated_issuance:
        if not required_issuer_secret and os.getenv("ALLOW_INSECURE_DEMO_ROLES", "true").lower() != "true":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Elevated role or non-default workspace issuance is disabled until SESSION_ISSUER_SECRET is configured.")
        if required_issuer_secret and (not issuer_secret or not hmac.compare_digest(issuer_secret, required_issuer_secret)):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="A valid X-Session-Issuer-Secret is required for elevated roles or non-default workspaces.")
    token, role, name, ws, exp = create_session_token(
        name=req.name,
        role=req.role,
        workspace_id=req.workspace_id,
    )
    return SessionResponse(
        token=token,
        role=role,
        name=name,
        workspace_id=ws,
        expires_at=exp,
    )


@router.get("/api/session/me", response_model=SessionUser)
def read_current_session(session: SessionUser = Depends(get_current_session)):
    """Returns the currently active session decoded from Authorization & X-Workspace-Id headers."""
    return session


========================================================================================
FILE: signing.py
========================================================================================
"""Ed25519 signing and SHA-256 hash-chain helpers for approval records."""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import hashlib
import json
import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import serialization
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import Ed25519PrivateKey, Ed25519PublicKey


def _load_private_key() -> Ed25519PrivateKey:
    """Load SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY (PEM or base64 32-byte seed), or create a dev key.

    Production deployments should set SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY to a persistent PEM
    private key or seed. A deterministic development fallback derived from the
    session secret keeps local restarts verifiable without generating key files.
    """
    configured = os.getenv("SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY", "").strip()
    if not configured:
        fallback_secret = (
            os.getenv("SIGNING_KEY_FALLBACK_SECRET")
            or os.getenv("SECRET_KEY")
            or os.getenv("SESSION_SECRET")
            or hashlib.sha256(os.getenv("DATABASE_URL", "").encode()).hexdigest()
        )
        return Ed25519PrivateKey.from_private_bytes(hashlib.sha256(fallback_secret.encode("utf-8")).digest())
    raw = configured.encode("utf-8")
    try:
        if "BEGIN" in configured:
            key = serialization.load_pem_private_key(raw, password=None)
            if not isinstance(key, Ed25519PrivateKey):
                raise ValueError("SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY must be an Ed25519 private key")
            return key
        decoded = base64.urlsafe_b64decode(configured + "=" * (-len(configured) % 4))
        if len(decoded) != 32:
            raise ValueError("base64 SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY must decode to a 32-byte Ed25519 seed")
        return Ed25519PrivateKey.from_private_bytes(decoded)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: {exc}") from exc


_PRIVATE_KEY = _load_private_key()
_PUBLIC_KEY = _PRIVATE_KEY.public_key()


def public_key_bytes() -> bytes:
    return _PUBLIC_KEY.public_bytes(
        encoding=serialization.Encoding.Raw,
        format=serialization.PublicFormat.Raw,
    )


def public_key_b64() -> str:
    return base64.b64encode(public_key_bytes()).decode("ascii")


def canonical_json(value: Dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), default=str).encode("utf-8")


def approval_entry_hash(
    *, scan_id: str, version_no: int, text_sha256: str, score: int,
    reviewer: str, role: str, prev_hash: Optional[str], created_at: str,
) -> str:
    record = {
        "scan_id": scan_id,
        "version_no": int(version_no),
        "text_sha256": text_sha256,
        "score": int(score),
        "reviewer": reviewer,
        "role": role,
        "prev_hash": prev_hash or "",
        "created_at": created_at,
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(record)).hexdigest()


def sign_entry_hash(entry_hash: str) -> str:
    return base64.b64encode(_PRIVATE_KEY.sign(bytes.fromhex(entry_hash))).decode("ascii")


def verify_entry_hash(entry_hash: str, signature: str, public_key: Optional[bytes] = None) -> bool:
    try:
        key = Ed25519PublicKey.from_public_bytes(public_key or public_key_bytes())
        key.verify(base64.b64decode(signature, validate=True), bytes.fromhex(entry_hash))
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


def utc_timestamp() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def ai_reply(candidates):
    body = {"choices": [{"message": {"content": json.dumps({"candidates": candidates})}}]}
    return 200, json.dumps(body)

def test_fallback_to_second_provider_when_first_model_is_retired(client, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("GROQ_API_KEY", "gsk_test")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GEMINI_API_KEY", "gem_test")
    calls = []

    def fake_post(url, headers, payload, timeout):
        calls.append((url, payload["model"]))
        if "groq" in url:
            return 404, '{"error":"model not found"}'
        return ai_reply([{"rule_id": 4, "flagged_text": "wipe out every competitor",
                          "explanation": "Aggressive tone.", "suggested_fix": "stand out in the market",
                          "confidence": 0.9, "claim_status": "Outdated Spec"}])

    monkeypatch.setattr(ai_client, "_post", fake_post)
    monkeypatch.setattr(ai_client, "_get", lambda *a, **k: (500, ""))
    text = "We will wipe out every competitor."
    r = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": text}).json()
    assert r["ai_status"] == "ok"
    assert "gemini" in r["ai_disclosure"]
    ai_issue = r["issues"][0]
    assert ai_issue["detected_by"] == "ai" and ai_issue["rule_id"] == 4
    assert text[ai_issue["start"]:ai_issue["end"]] == "wipe out every competitor"
    assert ai_issue["claim_status"] is None  # Tone rule: AI may not set claim_status
    assert any("groq" in c[0] for c in calls)  # it really did try Groq first


def test_dead_models_are_skipped_on_next_request(client, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("GROQ_API_KEY", "gsk_test")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GEMINI_API_KEY", "gem_test")
    groq_hits = []

    def fake_post(url, headers, payload, timeout):
        if "groq" in url:
            groq_hits.append(1)
            return 404, "gone"
        return ai_reply([])

    monkeypatch.setattr(ai_client, "_post", fake_post)
    monkeypatch.setattr(ai_client, "_get", lambda *a, **k: (500, ""))
    client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": "Hello there."})
    first = len(groq_hits)
    client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": "Hello again."})
    assert first > 0 and len(groq_hits) == first  # cooldown: no more wasted calls to the dead models

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- images
def png(color_bg, color_fg=None, size=(300, 200), mode="RGB"):
    img = Image.new(mode, size, color_bg)
    if color_fg:
        for x in range(60, 240):
            for y in range(80, 120):
                img.putpixel((x, y), color_fg)
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    img.save(buf, format="PNG")
    return buf.getvalue()


def scan_image(client, data):
    return client.post("/api/scan-image", files={"file": ("a.png", data, "image/png")})


def test_on_brand_high_contrast_image_has_no_color_issues(client):

    assert r["issues"] == []
    assert r["ai_status"] == "degraded"  # no vision key: text in the image was not checked, and it says so

    assert 9 in [i["rule_id"] for i in r["issues"]]

    contrast = [i for i in r["issues"] if i["rule_id"] == 10]
    assert contrast and contrast[0]["confidence"] < 1.0
    assert "contrast" in contrast[0]["suggested_fix"]

    assert not any("#000000" in i["flagged_text"] for i in r["issues"])

    monkeypatch.setenv("GEMINI_API_KEY", "gem_test")

    def fake_post(url, headers, payload, timeout):
        if isinstance(payload["messages"][1]["content"], list):  # the OCR call carries the image
            return 200, json.dumps({"choices": [{"message": {"content": json.dumps({"text": "Zero risk! Buy now"})}}]})

import sys
import os
# Force Python to look in the main backend directory for your modules
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..')))

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import main

import collab_router

import pdf_engine

# --- 1. RAG & DATABASE TESTS (These will pass immediately) ---
import pytest
from rag_verifier import extract_numbers_with_units, verify_claims, get_kb_version
from db import get_db_connection

def test_number_extraction():
    """Validates the deterministic regex unit extraction."""
    text = "The Pro Plan is $29/month and gets 110 km range in 45 minutes."
    nums = extract_numbers_with_units(text)
    assert "110km" in nums
    assert "$29/month" in nums
    assert "45minutes" in nums

def test_rag_verified_claim():
    """Tests if a valid factual claim correctly retrieves the KB chunk and verifies it."""
    result = verify_claims("Our certified battery range is 110 km under standard conditions.")
    assert len(result["verified_claims"]) > 0
    assert result["verified_claims"][0]["verdict"] == "Verified"
    assert result["verified_claims"][0]["similarity"] >= 0.72

def test_rag_contradicted_claim():
    """Tests if an invalid quantitative claim flags a High-Severity contradiction."""
    result = verify_claims("The ClaimLoom Pro Plan costs $15/month.")
    assert len(result["contradicted_issues"]) > 0
    issue = result["contradicted_issues"][0]
    assert issue["severity"] == "High"
    assert issue["claim_status"] == "Contradicted Fact"
    assert issue["evidence"]["verdict"] == "Contradicted"

def test_kb_versioning():
    """Validates the cache invalidation version fetcher."""
    version = get_kb_version("default")
    assert isinstance(version, int)
    assert version >= 1

@pytest.mark.parametrize("endpoint, expected_status", [
    ("/ping", 200),
    ("/health", 200),
])
def test_system_health(endpoint, expected_status):
    """The keep-alive and diagnostics routes return their documented contracts."""
    with TestClient(main.app) as client:
        response = client.get(endpoint)
    assert response.status_code == expected_status
    body = response.json()
    assert body.get("status") == "awake"
    if endpoint == "/ping":
        assert body.get("service") == "claimloom-backend-v3"
    else:
        assert "database" in body
        assert "ai_providers_configured" in body

@pytest.mark.parametrize("channel, input_text, expected_flag", [
    ("billboard", "This is way too much text for a billboard because it exceeds the twelve word maximum limit easily.", True),
    ("linkedin", "DM me to get the product update.", True),
    ("general", "Standard professional text goes here.", False),
])
def test_channel_rules(channel, input_text, expected_flag, monkeypatch):
    """Validates channel-specific constraints."""
    monkeypatch.setattr(ai_client, "AI_MODE", "rules_only")
    with TestClient(main.app) as client:
        response = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": input_text, "channel": channel})
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    data = response.json()
    channel_issues = [issue for issue in data["issues"] if issue.get("channel") == channel and issue.get("rule_id", 0) >= 1000]
    assert bool(channel_issues) is expected_flag

def test_ed25519_tamper_signature(monkeypatch):
    """A valid approval verifies, and a modified Ed25519 signature is rejected."""
    monkeypatch.setenv("ALLOW_INSECURE_DEMO_ROLES", "true")
    monkeypatch.delenv("SESSION_ISSUER_SECRET", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(ai_client, "AI_MODE", "rules_only")
    with TestClient(main.app) as client:
        session = client.post("/api/session", json={"name": "Round 3 Reviewer", "role": "Reviewer", "workspace_id": "default"})
        assert session.status_code == 200, session.text
        headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {session.json()['token']}"}
        scan = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": "Clear product information for enterprise teams."})
        assert scan.status_code == 200, scan.text
        scan_id = scan.json()["scan_id"]

        approved = client.post(f"/api/scans/{scan_id}/approve", json={}, headers=headers)
        assert approved.status_code == 200, approved.text
        assert approved.json()["approval"]["signature"]
        valid = client.get(f"/api/verify/{scan_id}")
        assert valid.status_code == 200, valid.text
        assert valid.json()["valid"] is True
        assert valid.json()["chain_valid"] is True

        approval = next(row for row in collab_router._approvals if row["scan_id"] == scan_id)
        original_signature = approval["signature"]
        try:
            approval["signature"] = "AAAA"
            tampered = client.get(f"/api/verify/{scan_id}")
            assert tampered.status_code == 200, tampered.text
            assert tampered.json()["valid"] is False
            assert tampered.json()["chain_valid"] is False
        finally:
            approval["signature"] = original_signature


def test_live_sample_pdf_scan_has_renderable_pages(monkeypatch):
    """The built-in brochure uses the real PDF pipeline and retains its bytes for previews."""
    monkeypatch.setattr(ai_client, "AI_MODE", "rules_only")

            "/api/scan-sample-pdf",
            data={"channel": "general", "workspace_id": "default"},
        )
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        result = response.json()
        assert result["input_type"] == "pdf"
        assert result["page_count"] == 2
        assert any(issue.get("detected_by") == "pdf_layout" for issue in result["issues"])
        preview = client.get(f"/api/scans/{result['scan_id']}/page/1.png")
        assert preview.status_code == 200
        assert preview.content.startswith(b"\x89PNG")
        redline = client.get(f"/api/scans/{result['scan_id']}/redline-pdf")
        assert redline.status_code == 200
        assert redline.content.startswith(b"%PDF")

    assert main._mem_scans["demo_scan_2"]["status"] == "approved"

    assert pdf_engine._mem_scan_json["demo_scan_2"]["reaudit"]["needed"] is True


def test_chunk3_price_update_syncs_rule5(monkeypatch):
    """Updating chunk 3 to $39/month must update Rule #5 so that:
    - Scanning 'The Pro Plan costs $39/month.' → zero Rule 5 violations.
    - Scanning 'The Pro Plan costs $29/month.' → Rule 5 flagged.
    After the test, reset Rule #5 back to $29/month to avoid polluting other tests.
    """
    import ai_client
    import db
    import ratelimit
    import seed_kb

    ratelimit.reset_rate_limits()
    monkeypatch.setattr(ai_client, "AI_MODE", "rules_only")

    with TestClient(main.app) as client:
        # Reset first so we start from a known clean state
        monkeypatch.setattr(db, "_connect", lambda: None)
        client.post("/api/admin/reset-demo")
        ratelimit.reset_rate_limits()

        # Update chunk 3 via PUT /api/kb/chunks/3
        put_res = client.put(
            "/api/kb/chunks/3",
            json={"chunk_text": "ClaimLoom Pro Plan subscription price is $39/month or $390/year."},
        )
        assert put_res.status_code == 200, put_res.text
        assert put_res.json()["affected_count"] >= 2

        # Scan "$39/month" — should be zero Rule 5 violations
        r39 = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": "The Pro Plan costs $39/month."})
        assert r39.status_code == 200, r39.text
        rule5_violations_39 = [i for i in r39.json()["issues"] if i.get("rule_id") == 5]
        assert len(rule5_violations_39) == 0, f"Expected no Rule 5 violations for $39/month after sync, got: {rule5_violations_39}"

        # Scan "$29/month" — should flag Rule 5 (old price is now wrong)
        r29 = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": "The Pro Plan costs $29/month."})
        assert r29.status_code == 200, r29.text
        rule5_violations_29 = [i for i in r29.json()["issues"] if i.get("rule_id") == 5]
        assert len(rule5_violations_29) > 0, f"Expected Rule 5 violation for $29/month after sync to $39, got none"

        # Reset demo to restore state
        client.post("/api/admin/reset-demo")


def test_extension_zip_download():
    """Verify that GET /api/extension/download returns a valid zip archive with extension assets."""
    import io
    import zipfile

    with TestClient(main.app) as client:
        response = client.get("/api/extension/download")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert "application/zip" in response.headers.get("content-type", "")
    assert response.content.startswith(b"PK")

    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(response.content)) as zf:
        namelist = zf.namelist()
        assert "manifest.json" in namelist
        assert "popup.js" in namelist


def test_kb_sources_endpoint():
    """Verify that GET /api/kb/sources returns chunks and kb_version."""
    with TestClient(main.app) as client:
        response = client.get("/api/kb/sources?workspace_id=default")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    data = response.json()
    assert "chunks" in data
    assert "kb_version" in data
    assert len(data["chunks"]) > 0
    assert "doc_title" in data["chunks"][0]
