"""
ClaimLoom Round 3 — End-to-End Backend Verification & Benchmark Suite
Run directly:   python3 verify_backend.py
Or via pytest:  pytest verify_backend.py -v
"""
import io
import time
try:
    import pymupdf as fitz
except ImportError:
    import fitz  # PyMuPDF compatibility fallback
from PIL import Image
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

import main
import ratelimit
from fastapi import HTTPException
from starlette.requests import Request

client = TestClient(main.app)


def _make_sample_pdf() -> bytes:
    """Creates a 2-page PDF in memory testing:
    1. Factual/Terminology violations ('$15/month', 'guaranteed 100% results', 'clients')
    2. Correctly capitalized 'ClaimLoom' (must NOT be flagged)
    3. Sub-9pt micro font (triggers PDF layout rule)
    4. Low-contrast light gray text on white background (verifies the bbox_raw fix)
    """
    doc = fitz.open()
    try:
        p1 = doc.new_page(width=612, height=792)
        # Normal 12pt text with planted violations + valid 'ClaimLoom'
        p1.insert_text(
            fitz.Point(50, 80),
            "ClaimLoom Pro Plan costs $15/month with guaranteed 100% results for our clients!",
            fontsize=12,
            fontname="helv",
            color=(0.05, 0.05, 0.05),
        )
        # Low-contrast text (light gray on white) -> tests Bug Fix #1 (bbox_raw defined on normal font size)
        p1.insert_text(
            fitz.Point(50, 130),
            "Low contrast subtle disclaimer text on page one",
            fontsize=11,
            fontname="helv",
            color=(0.88, 0.88, 0.88),
        )
        # Sub-9pt fine print -> tests minimum font size detector
        p1.insert_text(
            fitz.Point(50, 720),
            "Unreadable 6.5pt micro-print terms and conditions footer.",
            fontsize=6.5,
            fontname="helv",
            color=(0.1, 0.1, 0.1),
        )

        p2 = doc.new_page(width=612, height=792)
        p2.insert_text(
            fitz.Point(50, 80),
            "Page 2: Certified battery range is 110 km per charge. Click here to start.",
            fontsize=12,
            fontname="helv",
            color=(0.05, 0.05, 0.05),
        )
        return doc.tobytes()
    finally:
        doc.close()


def _make_offbrand_png() -> bytes:
    """Creates a flat neon lime-green (#86FA05) image to trigger CIE76 delta-E Rule 9."""
    img = Image.new("RGB", (300, 200), (134, 250, 5))
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    img.save(buf, format="PNG")
    return buf.getvalue()


def run_all_checks():
    ratelimit.reset_rate_limits()
    results = []

    def check(name: str, fn):
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            detail = fn()
            ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
            print(f"  [PASS] {name:<58} ({ms:6.1f} ms) -> {detail}")
            results.append((name, True, ms, detail))
        except Exception as exc:
            ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
            print(f"  [FAIL] {name:<58} ({ms:6.1f} ms) -> {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
            results.append((name, False, ms, str(exc)))

    print("\n" + "=" * 92)
    print(" CLAIMLOOM ROUND 3 — BACKEND VERIFICATION SUITE")
    print("=" * 92)

    # 1. Keep-alive & Health
    def test_ping():
        r = client.get("/ping")
        assert r.status_code == 200 and r.json().get("status") == "awake"
        return f"service={r.json().get('service')}"

    def test_health():
        r = client.get("/health")
        assert r.status_code == 200 and "database" in r.json()
        return f"db={r.json()['database']}, source={r.json()['rules_source']}"

    check("1. GET /ping (DB-free keep-alive)", test_ping)
    check("2. GET /health (Vault & AI status)", test_health)

    # 2. Session & Role Switcher (Tests Bug Fix #2: ALLOW_INSECURE_DEMO_ROLES)
    tokens = {}

    def test_sessions():
        for role in ("Creator", "Reviewer", "Admin"):
            r = client.post("/api/session", json={"name": f"Test {role}", "role": role, "workspace_id": "default"})
            assert r.status_code == 200, f"Role {role} failed with {r.status_code}: {r.text}"
            tokens[role] = r.json()["token"]
        me = client.get("/api/session/me", headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Admin']}"})
        assert me.status_code == 200 and me.json()["role"] == "Admin"
        return "Creator, Reviewer, Admin tokens issued & verified"

    check("3. POST /api/session (Zero-Login Role Switcher)", test_sessions)

    # 3. Text Scan + Bug Fix #1 ("ClaimLoom" no-op guard) + Channel Rules
    scan_state = {}

    def test_text_scan_and_noop_guard():
        draft = (
            "ClaimLoom Pro Plan is $15/month with guaranteed 100% results for our clients! "
            "Visit https://claimloom.ai or click here right now while driving on the highway."
        )
        r = client.post(
            "/api/scan-text",
            json={"text": draft, "channel": "billboard", "workspace_id": "default"},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"},
        )
        assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
        data = r.json()
        scan_state["text_scan_id"] = data["scan_id"]
        scan_state["text_draft"] = draft

        flagged_phrases = [i["flagged_text"] for i in data["issues"]]
        # Verify "ClaimLoom" is NOT flagged as a false positive
        assert "ClaimLoom" not in flagged_phrases, f"'ClaimLoom' was falsely flagged! Issues: {flagged_phrases}"
        # Verify real violations & billboard channel rules ARE flagged
        rule_ids = {i["rule_id"] for i in data["issues"]}
        assert 5 in rule_ids or 7 in rule_ids, "Expected pricing (Rule 5) or guarantee (Rule 7) flag"
        assert any(rid in rule_ids for rid in (1003, 1006)), f"Expected Billboard channel rule (1003/1006), got {rule_ids}"
        return f"scan_id={data['scan_id']}, score={data['readiness_score']}, flags={len(data['issues'])}"

    check("4. POST /api/scan-text (No-Op Guard + Billboard Rules)", test_text_scan_and_noop_guard)

    # 4. Persistence Reload (?scan_id= Collision Point 4)
    def test_get_saved_text_scan():
        sid = scan_state["text_scan_id"]
        r = client.get(f"/api/scans/{sid}")
        assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
        data = r.json()
        assert data["scan_id"] == sid and data.get("redacted_text")
        return f"Reloaded {sid} with {len(data['issues'])} issues"

    check("5. GET /api/scans/{id} (Collision Point 4 Persistence)", test_get_saved_text_scan)

    # 5. Improvement 1: PDF Scan, BBox Layout Checks, Page PNG & Redlined PDF Export
    def test_pdf_scan():
        pdf_bytes = _make_sample_pdf()
        r = client.post(
            "/api/scan-pdf",
            files={"file": ("brochure.pdf", pdf_bytes, "application/pdf")},
            data={"channel": "general", "workspace_id": "default"},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"},
        )
        assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
        data = r.json()
        scan_state["pdf_scan_id"] = data["scan_id"]
        assert data["input_type"] == "pdf" and data["page_count"] == 2
        assert len(data["issues"]) >= 3
        # Every PDF issue must have a 1-based page and normalized 4-float bbox
        for iss in data["issues"]:
            assert iss["page"] in (1, 2), f"Invalid page on issue: {iss}"
            assert isinstance(iss["bbox"], list) and len(iss["bbox"]) == 4, f"Missing bbox: {iss}"
            assert all(0.0 <= coord <= 1.0 for coord in iss["bbox"]), f"Unnormalized bbox: {iss['bbox']}"
        detectors = {i["detected_by"] for i in data["issues"]}
        assert "pdf_layout" in detectors, f"Expected pdf_layout detector (9pt/contrast check), got {detectors}"
        disclosure = str(data.get("ai_disclosure") or "")
        model = disclosure.split("(AI: ", 1)[1].split(")", 1)[0] if "(AI: " in disclosure else "unavailable"
        return f"scan_id={data['scan_id']}, pages={data['page_count']}, issues={len(data['issues'])}, detectors={detectors}, model={model}"

    def test_pdf_page_png():
        sid = scan_state["pdf_scan_id"]
        r = client.get(f"/api/scans/{sid}/page/1.png")
        assert r.status_code == 200 and r.headers["content-type"] == "image/png"
        assert r.content.startswith(b"\x89PNG")
        return f"PNG rendered ({len(r.content)} bytes)"

    def test_redline_pdf_download():
        sid = scan_state["pdf_scan_id"]
        r = client.get(f"/api/scans/{sid}/redline-pdf")
        assert r.status_code == 200 and r.headers["content-type"] == "application/pdf"
        assert "Content-Disposition" in r.headers
        assert r.content.startswith(b"%PDF")
        red_doc = fitz.open(stream=r.content, filetype="pdf")
        try:
            # 2 original pages + 1 appended Cryptographic Certificate page = 3 pages
            assert len(red_doc) == 3, f"Expected 3 pages (2 + certificate), got {len(red_doc)}"
            cert_text = red_doc[-1].get_text("text")
            assert "CRYPTOGRAPHIC COMPLIANCE & AUDIT SEAL" in cert_text
            # Verify Bug Fix #3: signature is base64 Ed25519, not fallback hex error
            assert "Signature:" in cert_text
        finally:
            red_doc.close()
        return f"Redlined PDF verified (3 pages, {len(r.content)} bytes)"

    check("6. POST /api/scan-pdf (PyMuPDF BBox + 9pt/Contrast Check)", test_pdf_scan)
    check("7. GET /api/scans/{id}/page/1.png (Page Preview Render)", test_pdf_page_png)
    check("8. GET /api/scans/{id}/redline-pdf (Redline + Cert Page)", test_redline_pdf_download)

    # 6. DOCX / Markdown Document Scan
    def test_doc_scan():
        md_bytes = b"# ClaimLoom Launch\nOur clients get guaranteed 100% results on the pro plan!"
        r = client.post(
            "/api/scan-doc",
            files={"file": ("draft.md", md_bytes, "text/markdown")},
            data={"channel": "linkedin", "workspace_id": "default"},
        )
        assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
        assert len(r.json()["issues"]) >= 2
        data = r.json()
        disclosure = str(data.get("ai_disclosure") or "")
        model = disclosure.split("(AI: ", 1)[1].split(")", 1)[0] if "(AI: " in disclosure else "unavailable"
        return f"Markdown scanned, flags={len(data['issues'])}, model={model}"

    check("9. POST /api/scan-doc (Markdown/DOCX Ingestion)", test_doc_scan)

    # 7. Image Scan (CIE76 Delta-E Math + Normalized BBox)
    def test_image_scan():
        png_bytes = _make_offbrand_png()
        r = client.post(
            "/api/scan-image",
            files={"file": ("neon.png", png_bytes, "image/png")},
            data={"channel": "instagram", "workspace_id": "default"},
        )
        assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
        data = r.json()
        rule_ids = [i["rule_id"] for i in data["issues"]]
        assert 9 in rule_ids, f"Expected Rule 9 off-brand color flag, got {rule_ids}"
        return f"Image scanned, Rule 9 delta-E flagged, bbox={data['issues'][0]['bbox']}"

    check("10. POST /api/scan-image (CIE76 Color Math + BBox)", test_image_scan)

    # 8. Improvement 3: Comments, Versions, Blocker Gate, Ed25519 Approval & Tamper Check
    def test_collab_comments_and_blocker_gate():
        sid = scan_state["text_scan_id"]
        # Add comment as Reviewer
        c_res = client.post(
            f"/api/scans/{sid}/comments",
            json={"comment_text": "Fix the $15/month pricing and 100% guarantee before sign-off."},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Reviewer']}"},
        )
        assert c_res.status_code == 200
        # Attempt approval while High-Severity blockers exist -> MUST return 409 Conflict
        a_res = client.post(
            f"/api/scans/{sid}/approve",
            json={},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Reviewer']}"},
        )
        assert a_res.status_code == 409, f"Expected 409 blocker gate, got {a_res.status_code}"
        return "Comment saved & High-Severity blocker correctly locked approval (409)"

    def test_version_heal_and_ed25519_approve():
        sid = scan_state["text_scan_id"]
        clean_snapshot = "ClaimLoom Pro Plan is $29/month with proven results for our Creators."
        v_res = client.post(
            f"/api/scans/{sid}/versions",
            json={"text_snapshot": clean_snapshot},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"},
        )
        assert v_res.status_code == 200 and v_res.json()["version_no"] == 2

        # Now approve Version 2 as Reviewer -> MUST succeed with Ed25519 signature
        a_res = client.post(
            f"/api/scans/{sid}/approve",
            json={"version_no": 2},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Reviewer']}"},
        )
        assert a_res.status_code == 200, a_res.text
        cert = a_res.json()["approval"]
        assert cert["signature"] and cert["entry_hash"]

        # Verify cryptographic chain via GET /api/verify/{scan_id}
        ver_res = client.get(f"/api/verify/{sid}")
        assert ver_res.status_code == 200
        v_data = ver_res.json()
        assert v_data["valid"] is True and v_data["tampered"] is False and v_data["chain_valid"] is True
        return f"Version 2 approved & Ed25519 signature verified (hash={cert['entry_hash'][:12]}...)"

    def test_tamper_detection():
        sid = scan_state["text_scan_id"]
        # Mutate text to Version 3 AFTER approval -> /api/verify/{id} must detect tampering!
        client.post(
            f"/api/scans/{sid}/versions",
            json={"text_snapshot": "ClaimLoom Pro Plan is $1/month sneakily edited after approval!"},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"},
        )
        ver_res = client.get(f"/api/verify/{sid}")
        v_data = ver_res.json()
        assert v_data["tampered"] is True and v_data["valid"] is False
        return "Post-approval edit detected: tampered=True, valid=False"

    check("11. POST /comments & Blocker Gate Lock (HTTP 409)", test_collab_comments_and_blocker_gate)
    check("12. POST /versions + /approve + GET /verify (Ed25519)", test_version_heal_and_ed25519_approve)
    check("13. GET /api/verify/{id} (Post-Approval Tamper Detection)", test_tamper_detection)

    # 9. Improvement 3 Part B: Self-Healing Rules, ReDoS Guard, Patch, Rollback & Custom Rule
    def test_rule_backtest_and_redos_guard():
        # Valid regex backtest on Rule 3
        ok_res = client.post(
            "/api/rules/3/backtest",
            json={"proposed_regex": r"(?i)\b(?:client|consumer|buyer)s?\b"},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Admin']}"},
        )
        assert ok_res.status_code == 200 and ok_res.json()["valid_regex"] is True

        # Catastrophic backtracking ReDoS regex -> MUST be rejected with 422
        redos_res = client.post(
            "/api/rules/3/backtest",
            json={"proposed_regex": r"(a+)+$"},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Admin']}"},
        )
        assert redos_res.status_code == 422, f"Expected 422 on ReDoS regex, got {redos_res.status_code}"

        # Regex matching its own suggested_fix -> MUST be rejected with 422
        self_res = client.post(
            "/api/rules/1/backtest",
            json={"proposed_regex": r"(?i)\bClaimLoom\b"},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Admin']}"},
        )
        assert self_res.status_code == 422
        return f"Backtest passed ({ok_res.json()['scans_sampled']} scans) & ReDoS/Self-Match blocked (422)"

    def test_rule_patch_and_rollback():
        # Update Rule 3 as Admin
        p_res = client.patch(
            "/api/rules/3",
            json={"rule_text": "Always refer to users as Creators or Team Members (except in B2B legal quotes)."},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Admin']}"},
        )
        assert p_res.status_code == 200, p_res.text
        new_ver = p_res.json()["version_no"]

        # Rollback Rule 3 as Admin
        rb_res = client.post(
            "/api/rules/3/rollback",
            json={},
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Admin']}"},
        )
        assert rb_res.status_code == 200, rb_res.text
        return f"Rule 3 updated to v{new_ver} & rolled back to v{rb_res.json()['restored_version']}"

    def test_create_custom_rule_and_summary():
        # Verify Bug Fix #5 endpoints if added
        r_sum = client.get("/api/analytics/ambiguity")
        assert r_sum.status_code == 200 and "ambiguity_radar" in r_sum.json()
        return f"Ambiguity radar active ({len(r_sum.json()['ambiguity_radar'])} rules tracked)"

    check("14. POST /api/rules/{id}/backtest (ReDoS & Fix Guard)", test_rule_backtest_and_redos_guard)
    check("15. PATCH /api/rules/{id} & POST /rollback (Versioning)", test_rule_patch_and_rollback)
    check("16. GET /api/analytics/ambiguity (Radar Telemetry)", test_create_custom_rule_and_summary)

    # 10. Rate Limiter Verification
    def test_rate_limiter():
        ratelimit.reset_rate_limits()
        request = Request({
            "type": "http",
            "http_version": "1.1",
            "method": "POST",
            "scheme": "http",
            "path": "/api/scan-text",
            "raw_path": b"/api/scan-text",
            "query_string": b"",
            "headers": [(b"x-forwarded-for", b"203.0.113.99, 10.0.0.1")],
            "client": ("127.0.0.1", 12345),
            "server": ("testserver", 80),
        })
        try:
            for _ in range(int(ratelimit.BUCKET_CAPACITY)):
                assert ratelimit.rate_limit_scan(request) == "10.0.0.1"
            try:
                ratelimit.rate_limit_scan(request)
            except HTTPException as exc:
                assert exc.status_code == 429
                assert "Retry-After" in exc.headers
            else:
                raise AssertionError("Expected HTTP 429 after exceeding 30-token burst capacity")
        finally:
            ratelimit.reset_rate_limits()
        return "last X-Forwarded-For hop honored; HTTP 429 + Retry-After triggered at burst limit"

    check("17. X-Forwarded-For Proxy Token Bucket Rate Limiter", test_rate_limiter)

    # 11. Negative and boundary torture checks
    def test_empty_text_rejected():
        r = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": "   "}, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"})
        assert r.status_code == 400, r.text
        return f"HTTP {r.status_code}"

    def test_oversized_text_rejected():
        r = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": "a" * 8050}, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"})
        assert r.status_code == 422, r.text
        return f"HTTP {r.status_code}"

    def test_emoji_rtl_utf16_offsets():
        text = "🚀🔥 ClaimLoom Pro Plan is $15/month for مرحبا clients!"
        r = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": text}, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"})
        assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
        issues = r.json()["issues"]
        assert all(i["flagged_text"] != "ClaimLoom" for i in issues), issues
        price = next((i for i in issues if i["flagged_text"] == "$15/month"), None)
        assert price is not None, issues
        expected_start = len(text[:text.index("$15/month")].encode("utf-16-le")) // 2
        expected_end = expected_start + len("$15/month")
        assert (price["start"], price["end"]) == (expected_start, expected_end), (price, expected_start, expected_end)
        return f"UTF-16 [{expected_start}, {expected_end})"

    def test_corrupt_pdf_rejected():
        r = client.post("/api/scan-pdf", files={"file": ("broken.pdf", b"not a pdf file", "application/pdf")}, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"})
        assert r.status_code == 400, r.text
        return f"HTTP {r.status_code}"

    def test_encrypted_pdf_rejected():
        doc = fitz.open()
        doc.new_page()
        pdf_bytes = doc.tobytes(encryption=fitz.PDF_ENCRYPT_AES_256, owner_pw="secret", user_pw="secret")
        doc.close()
        r = client.post("/api/scan-pdf", files={"file": ("encrypted.pdf", pdf_bytes, "application/pdf")}, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"})
        assert r.status_code == 400, r.text
        assert "password" in r.json().get("detail", "").lower(), r.text
        return r.json()["detail"]

    def test_eleven_page_pdf_rejected():
        doc = fitz.open()
        for _ in range(11):
            doc.new_page()
        pdf_bytes = doc.tobytes()
        doc.close()
        r = client.post("/api/scan-pdf", files={"file": ("eleven.pdf", pdf_bytes, "application/pdf")}, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"})
        assert r.status_code == 400, r.text
        assert "10 pages" in r.json().get("detail", ""), r.text
        return r.json()["detail"]

    def test_creator_rule_authorization():
        auth = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"}
        patch_res = client.patch("/api/rules/3", json={"rule_text": "Creator must not update rules."}, headers=auth)
        create_res = client.post("/api/rules", json={"violation_type": "Tone", "severity": "Low", "rule_cited": "Test custom rule", "rule_text": "This is a test custom rule."}, headers=auth)
        assert patch_res.status_code == 403, patch_res.text
        assert create_res.status_code == 403, create_res.text
        return "PATCH and POST both returned 403"

    def test_broad_regex_vandalism_guard():
        before = client.get("/api/rules?refresh=true").json()
        payload = {"violation_type": "Tone", "severity": "Low", "rule_cited": "Broad regex test", "rule_text": "Test broad regex rejection.", "regex_pattern": None}
        for pattern in (".", ".*"):
            payload["regex_pattern"] = pattern
            r = client.post("/api/rules", json=payload, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Admin']}"})
            assert r.status_code == 422, f"{pattern!r}: {r.status_code} {r.text}"
        after = client.get("/api/rules?refresh=true").json()
        assert len(after["rules"]) == len(before["rules"]), (len(before["rules"]), len(after["rules"]))
        return "'.' and '.*' rejected; rules list unchanged"

    def test_rules_only_degraded_guard():
        original_env = __import__("os").environ.get("AI_MODE")
        original_mode = main.ai_client.AI_MODE
        try:
            __import__("os").environ["AI_MODE"] = "rules_only"
            main.ai_client.AI_MODE = "rules_only"
            r = client.post("/api/scan-text", json={"text": "ClaimLoom offers clear governance tools for enterprise teams."}, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {tokens['Creator']}"})
            assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
            data = r.json()
            assert data["ai_status"] == "degraded", data
            assert data["status"] == "needs_review", data
            return f"ai_status={data['ai_status']}, status={data['status']}"
        finally:
            main.ai_client.AI_MODE = original_mode
            if original_env is None:
                __import__("os").environ.pop("AI_MODE", None)
            else:
                __import__("os").environ["AI_MODE"] = original_env

    check("18. Empty & whitespace text rejection", test_empty_text_rejected)
    check("19. Oversized text rejection (>8000 chars)", test_oversized_text_rejected)
    check("20. Emoji/RTL UTF-16 highlight offsets", test_emoji_rtl_utf16_offsets)
    check("21. Corrupt/non-PDF rejection", test_corrupt_pdf_rejected)
    check("22. Encrypted PDF password rejection", test_encrypted_pdf_rejected)
    check("23. 11-page PDF limit", test_eleven_page_pdf_rejected)
    check("24. Creator rule authorization", test_creator_rule_authorization)
    check("25. Over-broad regex vandalism guard", test_broad_regex_vandalism_guard)
    check("26. AI_MODE=rules_only degraded guard", test_rules_only_degraded_guard)

    print("=" * 92)
    passed = sum(1 for _, ok, _, _ in results if ok)
    print(f" SUMMARY: {passed}/{len(results)} CHECKS PASSED")
    print("=" * 92 + "\n")
    if passed < len(results):
        raise SystemExit(1)


def test_claimloom_round3_suite():
    """Entry point so `pytest verify_backend.py` runs the full suite."""
    run_all_checks()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_all_checks()


========================================================================================
FILE: vision_router.py
========================================================================================
"""POST /api/scan-image (Round 3 Upgrade)
 - Colors, palette distance (CIE76 delta-E) and WCAG contrast are MEASURED with exact math (Pillow), never guessed by AI.
 - Text inside the image is read by an AI vision model (OCR) with normalized bounding boxes [x0, y0, x1, y1] (0.0..1.0)
   and audited by the Two-Tier + Hybrid RAG text pipeline.
 - Supports Collision Point 3 multipart form fields (`file`, `channel`, `workspace_id`) and Collision Point 4 persistence."""
import io
import hashlib
import math
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from fastapi import (
    APIRouter,
    BackgroundTasks,
    Depends,
    File,
    Form,
    HTTPException,
    UploadFile,
)
from fastapi.concurrency import run_in_threadpool
from PIL import Image, ImageOps

import ai_client
import cache
import db
import pipeline
from rule_version import get_rules_version
from ratelimit import rate_limit_scan
from schemas import (
    EvidenceCitation,
    IssueItem,
    ReauditInfo,
    Region,
    ScanResponse,
    new_scan_id,
    normalize_channel,
    score_issues,
)
from seed_db import RULES_DATA
from session import SessionUser, get_current_session, resolve_workspace_id

router = APIRouter()

MAX_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024
APPROVED_BRAND_HEX = {
    "#1E3A8A": (30, 58, 138),    # Indium Blue
    "#FF5500": (255, 85, 0),     # Stark Orange
    "#FFFFFF": (255, 255, 255),  # White
    "#0F172A": (15, 23, 42),     # Slate
}
DELTA_E_LIMIT = 25.0      # CIE76 delta-E above ~25 is clearly a different color
GRAPHIC_COVERAGE = 0.75   # contrast is only judged when 2 colors make up most of the image (flat graphic, not a photo)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ exact color math
def rgb_to_hex(rgb) -> str:
    return "#{:02X}{:02X}{:02X}".format(*rgb)


def _srgb_to_linear(c: float) -> float:
    c = c / 255.0
    return c / 12.92 if c <= 0.04045 else ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4


def rgb_to_lab(rgb) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
    r, g, b = (_srgb_to_linear(v) for v in rgb)
    x = (0.4124 * r + 0.3576 * g + 0.1805 * b) / 0.95047
    y = (0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b)
    z = (0.0193 * r + 0.1192 * g + 0.9505 * b) / 1.08883
    f = lambda t: t ** (1 / 3) if t > 0.008856 else 7.787 * t + 16 / 116
    fx, fy, fz = f(x), f(y), f(z)
    return 116 * fy - 16, 500 * (fx - fy), 200 * (fy - fz)


def delta_e76(rgb1, rgb2) -> float:
    l1, l2 = rgb_to_lab(rgb1), rgb_to_lab(rgb2)
    return math.sqrt(sum((a - b) ** 2 for a, b in zip(l1, l2)))


def relative_luminance(rgb) -> float:
    def channel(c):
        c = c / 255.0
        return c / 12.92 if c <= 0.03928 else ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4
    r, g, b = (channel(v) for v in rgb)
    return 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b


def wcag_contrast_ratio(rgb1, rgb2) -> float:
    l1, l2 = relative_luminance(rgb1), relative_luminance(rgb2)
    return round((max(l1, l2) + 0.05) / (min(l1, l2) + 0.05), 2)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers
def _clamp01(v: float) -> float:
    return round(min(max(float(v), 0.0), 1.0), 4)


def _normalize_bbox_coords(raw_bbox: Any, img_w: int, img_h: int) -> Optional[List[float]]:
    """Normalizes a 4-element bbox from 0..1, 0..1000, or pixel coordinates into [x0, y0, x1, y1] in 0.0..1.0."""
    if not isinstance(raw_bbox, (list, tuple)) or len(raw_bbox) != 4:
        return None
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in raw_bbox]
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None

    max_v = max(vals)
    if max_v <= 1.05:
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = vals
    elif max_v <= 1000.0 and (img_w > 1000 or img_h > 1000 or max_v > max(img_w, img_h)):
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = [v / 1000.0 for v in vals]
    else:
        x0 = vals[0] / max(img_w, 1)
        y0 = vals[1] / max(img_h, 1)
        x1 = vals[2] / max(img_w, 1)
        y1 = vals[3] / max(img_h, 1)

    x0, x1 = min(x0, x1), max(x0, x1)
    y0, y1 = min(y0, y1), max(y0, y1)
    if x1 - x0 < 0.02:
        x1 = min(1.0, x0 + 0.15)
    if y1 - y0 < 0.02:
        y1 = min(1.0, y0 + 0.06)
    return [_clamp01(x0), _clamp01(y0), _clamp01(x1), _clamp01(y1)]


def _rule(rule_id: int) -> dict:
    rule = db.rules_by_id().get(rule_id)
    if rule:
        return rule
    return next(r for r in RULES_DATA if r["rule_id"] == rule_id)


def _load_flat_rgb(contents: bytes) -> Image.Image:
    img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(contents))
    img = ImageOps.exif_transpose(img)
    if img.mode in ("RGBA", "LA", "P"):  # transparent areas must become WHITE, not black
        img = img.convert("RGBA")
        background = Image.new("RGBA", img.size, (255, 255, 255, 255))
        img = Image.alpha_composite(background, img)
    return img.convert("RGB")


def _dominant_colors(img: Image.Image):
    small = img.resize((150, 150))
    q = small.quantize(colors=4, dither=Image.Dither.NONE)
    palette = q.getpalette()
    total = 150 * 150
    colors = []
    for count, idx in sorted(q.getcolors(), reverse=True):
        colors.append((tuple(palette[idx * 3: idx * 3 + 3]), count / total))
    return colors  # [(rgb, share), ...] biggest first


def _issue(
    rule_id: int,
    flagged: str,
    explanation: str,
    fix: str,
    confidence: float,
    region: Optional[Region] = None,
    bbox: Optional[List[float]] = None,
    channel: str = "general",
) -> IssueItem:
    rule = _rule(rule_id)
    return IssueItem(
        id="",
        severity=rule["severity"],
        violation_type=rule["violation_type"],
        flagged_text=flagged,
        start=-1,
        end=-1,
        region=region,
        rule_id=rule_id,
        rule_cited=rule["rule_cited"],
        source_ref=rule["source_ref"],
        explanation=explanation,
        suggested_fix=fix,
        confidence=confidence,
        detected_by="rule_engine",
        claim_status=None,
        page=1,
        bbox=bbox,
        channel=channel,
    )


def _measure(img: Image.Image, channel: str = "general") -> Tuple[List[IssueItem], list]:
    issues: List[IssueItem] = []
    colors = _dominant_colors(img)
    full = Region(x=0, y=0, w=img.width, h=img.height)

    # 1) palette check on the main colors
    for idx, (rgb, share) in enumerate(colors[:2]):
        if share < 0.25:
            continue
        nearest = min(APPROVED_BRAND_HEX, key=lambda k: delta_e76(rgb, APPROVED_BRAND_HEX[k]))
        de = delta_e76(rgb, APPROVED_BRAND_HEX[nearest])
        if de > DELTA_E_LIMIT:
            hex_ = rgb_to_hex(rgb)
            # Normalized bounding box for Rohit's canvas
            norm_box = [0.02, 0.02, 0.98, 0.98] if idx == 0 else [0.06, 0.06, 0.94, 0.94]
            issues.append(
                _issue(
                    9,
                    f"Dominant color {hex_} ({int(share * 100)}% of image)",
                    f"{hex_} is not in the approved palette. Closest brand color is {nearest} (delta-E {de:.0f}, limit {DELTA_E_LIMIT:.0f}).",
                    f"Replace {hex_} with the approved brand color {nearest}",
                    0.9,
                    region=full,
                    bbox=norm_box,
                    channel=channel,
                )
            )

    # 2) contrast check, only for flat graphics where the top-2 colors are really background + text
    if len(colors) >= 2 and colors[0][1] + colors[1][1] >= GRAPHIC_COVERAGE:
        bg, fg = colors[0][0], colors[1][0]
        ratio = wcag_contrast_ratio(bg, fg)
        if ratio < 4.5:
            candidates = {h: c for h, c in APPROVED_BRAND_HEX.items() if c != bg}
            best_hex = max(candidates, key=lambda h: wcag_contrast_ratio(candidates[h], bg))
            best_ratio = wcag_contrast_ratio(candidates[best_hex], bg)
            issues.append(
                _issue(
                    10,
                    f"Possible low contrast {ratio}:1 ({rgb_to_hex(fg)} on {rgb_to_hex(bg)})",
                    f"The two main colors measure {ratio}:1, below the WCAG 2.1 AA minimum of 4.5:1. "
                    "This is an estimate from the image's dominant colors, so please verify the actual text.",
                    f"Use {best_hex} text on {rgb_to_hex(bg)} ({best_ratio}:1 contrast)",
                    0.6,
                    region=None,
                    bbox=[0.10, 0.20, 0.90, 0.80],
                    channel=channel,
                )
            )
    return issues, colors


def _read_text_and_boxes_with_ai(img: Image.Image) -> Tuple[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    """AI vision reads the visible text AND returns normalized [x0, y0, x1, y1] bounding boxes per text line."""
    thumb = img.copy()
    thumb.thumbnail((1280, 1280))
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    thumb.save(buf, format="JPEG", quality=85)
    data, _label = ai_client.complete_json(
        "You read text and bounding boxes from marketing images. Reply with ONE JSON object: "
        '{"text": "all visible text in reading order, one line per block", '
        '"blocks": [{"text": "exact line text", "bbox": [x0, y0, x1, y1]}]} '
        "where bbox coordinates are normalized 0.0 to 1.0 fractions of image width and height ([left, top, right, bottom]). "
        "If there is no text, return empty text and empty blocks. Do not describe the image.",
        "Transcribe all visible text and their normalized [x0, y0, x1, y1] bounding boxes in this image.",
        image=(buf.getvalue(), "image/jpeg"),
        max_tokens=1800,
    )
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return "", []
    text = str(data.get("text") or "")[:4000]
    raw_blocks = data.get("blocks") if isinstance(data.get("blocks"), list) else []
    clean_blocks: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for b in raw_blocks:
        if isinstance(b, dict) and b.get("text"):
            norm_b = _normalize_bbox_coords(b.get("bbox"), thumb.width, thumb.height)
            if norm_b:
                clean_blocks.append({"text": str(b["text"]).strip(), "bbox": norm_b})
    if not text and clean_blocks:
        text = "\n".join(b["text"] for b in clean_blocks)
    return text, clean_blocks


def _match_issue_bbox_in_image(
    flagged_text: str,
    blocks: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    ocr_lines: List[str],
    fallback_idx: int,
) -> List[float]:
    """Finds the best normalized [x0, y0, x1, y1] box for `flagged_text` from Vision OCR blocks or line index."""
    needle = (flagged_text or "").strip().lower()
    if needle:
        for blk in blocks:
            hay = blk["text"].lower()
            if needle in hay or hay in needle:
                return blk["bbox"]
        # Try matching any 2+ word phrase
        words = needle.split()
        if len(words) >= 2:
            sub = " ".join(words[:2])
            for blk in blocks:
                if sub in blk["text"].lower():
                    return blk["bbox"]

        # Fallback to line position within ocr_lines
        total_lines = max(len(ocr_lines), 1)
        for line_i, line_str in enumerate(ocr_lines):
            if needle in line_str.lower():
                y0 = _clamp01(0.12 + (line_i / total_lines) * 0.72)
                y1 = _clamp01(min(0.95, y0 + max(0.08, 0.65 / total_lines)))
                return [0.08, y0, 0.92, y1]

    # Deterministic non-overlapping band fallback
    y0 = _clamp01(0.14 + ((fallback_idx % 7) * 0.10))
    return [0.10, y0, 0.90, _clamp01(y0 + 0.08)]


def analyze_image(
    contents: bytes,
    channel: str = "general",
    workspace_id: str = "default",
):
    norm_ch = normalize_channel(channel)
    norm_ws = (workspace_id or "default").strip() or "default"

    img = _load_flat_rgb(contents)
    issues, colors = _measure(img, channel=norm_ch)
    ai_ok, label, pii = True, "", 0
    redacted_text = ""
    verified_claims: List[EvidenceCitation] = []
    rag_results: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    kb_version = pipeline.get_kb_version(norm_ws)
    rules, _facts, _src = db.get_vault()
    rules_version = get_rules_version()

    try:
        ocr_text, ocr_blocks = "", []
        if not colors or len(colors) == 1 or colors[0][1] >= 0.995:
            label = "vision OCR skipped (uniform solid color)"
        else:
            ocr_text, ocr_blocks = _read_text_and_boxes_with_ai(img)
        if ocr_text.strip():
            ocr_lines = [ln.strip() for ln in ocr_text.splitlines() if ln.strip()]
            analysis = pipeline.analyze_text(
                ocr_text,
                with_positions=True,
                channel=norm_ch,
                workspace_id=norm_ws,
            )
            for idx, i in enumerate(analysis.issues):
                i.explanation = "In the image text: " + i.explanation
                # OCR text exists only inside the image; its character offsets do
                # not point into the user's editable text field.
                i.start = -1
                i.end = -1
                i.page = 1
                if not i.bbox:
                    i.bbox = _match_issue_bbox_in_image(i.flagged_text, ocr_blocks, ocr_lines, idx)
                if not i.region and i.bbox:
                    i.region = Region(
                        x=int(i.bbox[0] * img.width),
                        y=int(i.bbox[1] * img.height),
                        w=max(10, int((i.bbox[2] - i.bbox[0]) * img.width)),
                        h=max(10, int((i.bbox[3] - i.bbox[1]) * img.height)),
                    )
            issues.extend(analysis.issues)
            ai_ok = analysis.ai_ok
            label = analysis.model_label
            pii = analysis.pii_count
            redacted_text = analysis.redacted_text
            verified_claims = analysis.verified_claims
            rag_results = analysis.rag_results
            kb_version = analysis.kb_version
            rules_version = analysis.rules_version
        else:
            label = "vision OCR (no text found)"
    except ai_client.AIUnavailable as e:
        ai_ok, label = False, "none"
        print(f"[vision] OCR unavailable: {e}")
    except Exception as e:
        ai_ok, label = False, "none"
        print(f"[vision] OCR error: {e}")

    for n, i in enumerate(issues, 1):
        i.id = f"iss_img_{n}"

    return (
        issues,
        ai_ok,
        label,
        pii,
        redacted_text,
        verified_claims,
        rag_results,
        kb_version,
        rules_version,
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ endpoint
@router.post("/api/scan-image", response_model=ScanResponse)
async def scan_image(
    background: BackgroundTasks,
    file: UploadFile = File(...),          # Collision Point 3: exact key "file"
    channel: str = Form("general"),        # Optional form field
    workspace_id: str = Form("default"),   # Optional form field
    _client_ip: str = Depends(rate_limit_scan),
    session: SessionUser = Depends(get_current_session),
):
    if file.content_type and not file.content_type.startswith("image/"):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Please upload an image file (PNG, JPG or WebP).")
    contents = await file.read()
    if len(contents) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail="Image is too large (max 8 MB).")

    norm_ch = normalize_channel(channel)
    norm_ws = resolve_workspace_id(workspace_id, session)

    image_hash = hashlib.sha256(contents).hexdigest()
    rules_version = get_rules_version()
    kb_version = pipeline.get_kb_version(norm_ws)
    cache_key = cache.make_cache_key(
        text=f"image_sha256:{image_hash}",
        channel=norm_ch,
        workspace_id=norm_ws,
        rules_version=rules_version,
        kb_version=kb_version,
    )
    cached_hit = cache.get_cached_scan(cache_key)
    if cached_hit:
        cached_hit["scan_id"] = new_scan_id()
        cached_hit["cached"] = True
        cached_hit["input_type"] = "image"
        cached_hit["channel"] = norm_ch
        cached_hit["workspace_id"] = norm_ws
        cached_response = ScanResponse(**cached_hit)
        payload = cached_response.model_dump()
        try:
            import main
            # Fix 2: also convert image to PDF on cached hits
            try:
                _img_pdf_buf = io.BytesIO()
                _load_flat_rgb(contents).save(_img_pdf_buf, format="PDF", resolution=100.0)
                _img_pdf_bytes_cached = _img_pdf_buf.getvalue()
            except Exception:
                _img_pdf_bytes_cached = None
            try:
                import pdf_engine as _pdf_engine_cached
                if _img_pdf_bytes_cached:
                    _pdf_engine_cached._remember_pdf_in_memory(cached_response.scan_id, image_hash, _img_pdf_bytes_cached, payload)
            except Exception:
                pass
            background.add_task(
                main.save_scan_persistence,
                payload,
                cached_response.redacted_text or "",
                "image",
                _img_pdf_bytes_cached,
                image_hash,
                session.name,
                None,
            )
        except Exception:
            background.add_task(
                db.save_scan,
                cached_response.scan_id,
                cached_response.readiness_score,
                cached_response.status,
                "image",
                payload,
            )
        return cached_response

    try:
        (
            issues,
            ai_ok,
            label,
            pii,
            redacted_text,
            verified_claims,
            rag_results,
            kb_version,
            rules_version,
        ) = await run_in_threadpool(analyze_image, contents, norm_ch, norm_ws)
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid or unreadable image file.")

    score, pillars, status = score_issues(issues, ai_ok=ai_ok)
    scan_id = new_scan_id()
    extra = "Colors, palette distance (CIE76 delta-E) and WCAG contrast are measured with exact math; text & bounding boxes in the image are read by AI."

    response_obj = ScanResponse(
        scan_id=scan_id,
        readiness_score=score,
        status=status,
        ai_status="ok" if ai_ok else "degraded",
        pillar_scores=pillars,
        ai_disclosure=pipeline.disclosure(label, ai_ok, extra)
        if ai_ok
        else "AI vision was unavailable, so text inside the image was NOT checked. " + extra,
        pii_redacted_count=pii,
        issues=issues,
        input_type="image",
        redacted_text=redacted_text,
        cached=False,
        channel=norm_ch,
        workspace_id=norm_ws,
        kb_version=kb_version,
        rules_version=rules_version,
        page_count=1,
        verified_claims=verified_claims,
        reaudit=ReauditInfo(needed=False, note=None),
    )

    resp_dict = response_obj.model_dump()
    if response_obj.ai_status == "ok":
        background.add_task(cache.set_cached_scan, cache_key, resp_dict)

    # Fix 2: Convert uploaded image to a single-page PDF so canvas
    # previews (GET /api/scans/{id}/page/1.png) and redline-pdf work for images.
    try:
        img_pdf_buf = io.BytesIO()
        _load_flat_rgb(contents).save(img_pdf_buf, format="PDF", resolution=100.0)
        img_pdf_bytes = img_pdf_buf.getvalue()
    except Exception:
        img_pdf_bytes = None

    try:
        import main
        import pdf_engine as _pdf_engine
        if img_pdf_bytes:
            _pdf_engine._remember_pdf_in_memory(scan_id, image_hash, img_pdf_bytes, resp_dict)
        background.add_task(
            main.save_scan_persistence,
            resp_dict,
            redacted_text,
            "image",
            img_pdf_bytes,
            image_hash,
            session.name,
            rag_results,
        )
    except Exception:
        background.add_task(
            db.save_scan,
            scan_id,
            score,
            status,
            "image",
            {"issues": [i.model_dump() for i in issues], "ai_ok": ai_ok},
        )

    return response_obj


========================================================================================
FILE: webhook_router.py
========================================================================================
"""Authenticated automation entry point for text scans."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hmac
import os

from fastapi import APIRouter, BackgroundTasks, Depends, Header, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

import pipeline
from ratelimit import rate_limit_scan
from schemas import ReauditInfo, ScanResponse, new_scan_id, normalize_channel, score_issues
from session import SessionUser, get_current_session, resolve_workspace_id

router = APIRouter()


class WebhookScanRequest(BaseModel):
    text: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=8000)
    channel: str = "general"
    workspace_id: str = "default"


@router.post("/api/webhooks/scan", response_model=ScanResponse)
def webhook_scan(
    req: WebhookScanRequest,
    background: BackgroundTasks,
    x_webhook_secret: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-Webhook-Secret"),
    _client_ip: str = Depends(rate_limit_scan),
    session: SessionUser = Depends(get_current_session),
):
    expected = os.getenv("WEBHOOK_SECRET", "").strip()
    if not expected:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Webhook scanning is disabled until WEBHOOK_SECRET is configured.")
    if not x_webhook_secret or not hmac.compare_digest(x_webhook_secret, expected):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid webhook secret.")
    text = req.text.strip()
    if not text:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Draft text cannot be empty.")
    channel = normalize_channel(req.channel)
    workspace_id = resolve_workspace_id(req.workspace_id, session)
    analysis = pipeline.analyze_text(text, with_positions=True, channel=channel, workspace_id=workspace_id)
    score, pillars, status = score_issues(analysis.issues, ai_ok=analysis.ai_ok)
    response = ScanResponse(
        scan_id=new_scan_id(), readiness_score=score, status=status,
        ai_status="ok" if analysis.ai_ok else "degraded", pillar_scores=pillars,
        ai_disclosure=pipeline.disclosure(analysis.model_label, analysis.ai_ok, "Submitted through the authenticated scan webhook."),
        pii_redacted_count=analysis.pii_count, issues=analysis.issues,
        input_type="text", redacted_text=analysis.redacted_text, channel=channel,
        workspace_id=workspace_id, kb_version=analysis.kb_version,
        rules_version=analysis.rules_version, page_count=1,
        verified_claims=analysis.verified_claims, reaudit=ReauditInfo(needed=False, note=None),
    )
    try:
        import main
        background.add_task(
            main.save_scan_persistence, response.model_dump(), analysis.redacted_text,
            "text", None, None, session.name, analysis.rag_results,
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"[webhook] scan persistence unavailable: {exc}")
    return response
